The multimodal "assessment + optimization" paradigm offers a transferable, sustainable path to culturally responsive, differentiated spatial design amid global homogenization.

</details>


### [66] [EMAS: Stabilizing Multi-Agent System Evolution through Evidence-Guided Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07196)
*Chao Fei,Qingyi Si,Kaihua Liang,Yanghua Xiao,Panos Kalnis,Hongcheng Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EMAS是一种基于经验演化多智能体系统的框架，通过将运行轨迹转化为结构化诊断，仅在相同诊断反复出现时生成候选修订，并通过配对验证确保修订有效，从而在不更新LLM参数的前提下同时优化智能体拓扑和提示词，兼顾准确率与成本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化多智能体系统设计方法通常只在一个初始设计阶段优化提示词和拓扑，之后对新样本部署时保持不变。问题在于：(1) 运行中积累的经验没有被整合为可复用的系统更新；(2) 追求精度的设计可能带来高昂的token成本。因此需要一种能利用样本经验持续改进MAS拓扑和提示词的框架。

Method: EMAS的核心流程包括：(1) 将MAS运行轨迹转换为结构化诊断，诊断内容包含具体的修订操作和目标；(2) 仅当相同的诊断在多个样本中重复出现时才生成候选修订；(3) 通过将候选修订与当前MAS进行配对验证，满足对应验收标准后才应用修订；(4) 整个过程只更新拓扑和提示词，不修改LLM参数。循环执行演化epoch即可持续改进。

Result: 在四个基准和两个LLM上，EMAS在两个骨干模型上均取得了最高的任务加权总体准确率，并在8个模型-基准组合中的6个达到最优或并列最优。在两个演化epoch内，EMAS在Kimi-K2-6和Qwen3.6-27B上分别取得了6.30%和20.10%的任务加权准确率相对提升。在MBPP + Qwen3.6-27B设置下，准确率从55.09%提升至89.12%，同时每个任务的token使用量降低了62.2%。

Conclusion: EMAS证明了将新样本中获得的经验转化为MAS拓扑和提示词的可复用更新是可行的，能够同时实现更高的准确率和更低的成本，为多智能体系统的持续演化提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Many methods for automated multi-agent system design optimize prompts and topologies during an initial design stage and then deploy the resulting system unchanged on subsequent samples. Experience from these samples is rarely consolidated into reusable system updates, while accuracy-oriented designs may incur high token costs. We introduce EMAS (Evolving Multi-Agent System), which uses this experience to revise MAS topology and prompts without updating LLM parameters, either to improve accuracy or to reduce cost. EMAS converts traces into structured diagnoses that specify a revision operation and target. It generates a candidate revision only when the same diagnosis recurs across samples and applies it only if paired validation against the current MAS meets the corresponding acceptance criterion. Across four benchmarks and two LLMs, EMAS attains the highest task-weighted overall accuracy for both backbones and is best or tied in six of eight model--benchmark settings. Within two evolution epochs, EMAS achieves relative gains of 6.30% and 20.10% in task-weighted accuracy on Kimi-K2-6 and Qwen3.6-27B, respectively. On MBPP with Qwen3.6-27B, EMAS raises accuracy from 55.09% to 89.12% while reducing token use per task by 62.2%. These results show that EMAS can turn experience from new samples into reusable updates to MAS topology and prompts.

</details>


### [67] [Authoring and Management of Transparent Research Integrity Assessments of Randomised Clinical Trial Publications Using LLM-assisted Tools and Provenance Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07202)
*Milan Markovic,Goutham Indukuri,Somayajulu Sripada,Colby J. Vorland,Jack Wilkinson,Clare Robertson,Mark Bolland,Andrew Grey,Miriam Brazzelli,Alison Avenell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了INSPECT-AI，一个基于大语言模型的交互式工具，用于辅助人类审稿人基于INSPECT-SR框架和RIPE-O本体对已发表的随机对照试验进行科研诚信评估，并构建了包含140条专家评估的RIPE-KG知识图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机对照试验的系统综述是临床护理指南的常规证据来源，需满足高科研诚信标准以防止低质量或虚假研究影响临床实践。然而，已发表RCT的科研诚信评估过程复杂、依赖人工，且不同评估者可能产生不同观点，亟需自动化工具辅助。

Method: 构建了LLM驱动的交互式工具INSPECT-AI，基于社区认可的INSPECT-SR框架执行科研诚信评估；利用RIPE-O本体记录评估过程的可溯源信息；并在此基础上生成包含140条专家评估（覆盖95篇RCT出版物）的RIPE-KG知识图谱。

Result: 生成了RIPE-KG知识图谱，包含来自95篇RCT出版物的140条专家科研诚信评估记录，全部由INSPECT-AI生成并以RIPE-O本体描述。

Conclusion: INSPECT-AI结合LLM、结构化评估框架和本体驱动的溯源记录，为科研诚信评估提供了可交互、可溯源的辅助工具，并初步验证了其在真实RCT数据集上的可行性。

Abstract: Systematic reviews of Randomised Controlled Trials (RCTs) are routinely used as evidence for clinical care guidelines. Such evidence has to meet high research integrity standards to prevent low quality or false research outputs influencing the clinical care. However, assessing research integrity of published RCTs is a complex process requiring manual effort, and potentially resulting in diverse opinions of the human assessors. This paper describes INSPECT-AI, an LLM-based interactive tool that assists human reviewers with research integrity assessments of published RCTs based on the community approved INSPECT-SR framework, and the Research Integrity Provenance and Evidence ontology (RIPE-O) for documenting the provenance of the assessment process. In addition, we present the Research Integrity Provenance and Evidence knowledge graph (RIPE-KG), an initial set of 140 expert research integrity assessments of 95 RCT publications generated by INSPECT-AI and described using RIPE-O.

</details>


### [68] [Beyond the Black Box: Interpretable Models of Human Randomisation Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07220)
*Ngoc Linh Dao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文使用84,060个来自2,802对参与者的决策数据，研究O'Neill零和纸牌游戏中人类玩家的行为偏离（非i.i.d.随机选择），比较了朴素模型、行为模型、可解释机器学习与深度学习模型的预测能力，并基于LASSO诊断提出嵌套频率追踪扩展。结果表明重复/规避行为（尤其是玩家对自身近期动作历史的管理）贡献了大部分可解释且可策略性利用的信号，而频率追踪对样本外预测贡献甚微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合策略均衡预测i.i.d.行为，但人类玩家系统性偏离该基准，且这种偏离可由LSTM等黑箱序列模型预测。作者希望探究能否用透明（可解释）的替代模型实现同等预测能力，同时揭示背后的行为结构。

Method: 使用84,060个决策（2,802对参与者）数据，首先将朴素模型与行为模型基准与可解释机器学习和深度学习模型进行比较；随后将先前研究中的修改版EWA规格与这些基准进行评估；并利用LASSO诊断来推动进一步开发嵌套的频率追踪扩展。

Result: 重复或避免行为，尤其是玩家对自身最近动作历史的管理，解释了大部分可解释且具有策略利用价值的信号；频率追踪在样本外预测中几乎没有额外贡献。

Conclusion: 透明模型能够捕捉LSTM等黑箱模型的预测力，行为结构主要由玩家对自身近期动作历史的重复/规避管理驱动，而非频率追踪机制。

Abstract: Mixed strategy equilibrium predicts i.i.d play: past actions should not help predict future decisions. Human players, however, systematically depart from this benchmark, and in O'Neill's zero sum card game, these departures can be predicted by black box sequence models such as LSTMs. This paper asks whether that predictive power can be achieved by transparent alternatives that also reveal the behavioural structure behind it. Using 84,060 decisions from 2,802 pairs, the analysis first benchmarks naive and behavioral models against interpretable machine learning and deep learning models, then evaluates the modified EWA specifications of prior work against these benchmarks and uses the LASSO diagnostics to motivate a further nested frequency tracking extension. The results show that repeat or avoid behavior, especially players' management of their own recent action histories, accounts for most of the interpretable and strategically exploitable signal, while frequency tracking adds little out of sample.

</details>


### [69] [From probability to causality in probabilistic logic programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07230)
*Zora Wurm,Kilian Rückschloß,Felix Weitkämper*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了概率逻辑编程中的因果顺序可识别性问题，探讨了何时从学习到的概率分布中能唯一确定因果顺序，以确保干预推理的语义清晰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当从数据中学习概率逻辑程序的结构时，仅使用概率信息，而同一概率分布可能兼容多种因果顺序，这导致干预推理产生歧义。因此需要确定在什么条件下因果顺序能被分布唯一确定。

Method: 利用无环概率逻辑程序与贝叶斯网络之间的对应关系，推导出概率信息唯一确定因果顺序的条件；同时通过考虑底层关系词汇表所引入的因果对称性约束，纳入关系结构约束，最终提出验证学习到的概率逻辑程序是否支持良好定义的干预语义的方法。

Result: 得出了在什么条件下学习到的概率逻辑程序中的概率信息能唯一确定因果顺序的充分条件，并提出了结合关系结构约束（因果对称性）的验证方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法可以验证学习到的概率逻辑程序是否具有唯一确定的因果顺序，从而支持良好定义的干预（因果关系）推理语义。

Abstract: Probabilistic logic programming is a formalism of statistical relational artificial intelligence that supports causal queries, including interventions from outside the system. When the structure of a probabilistic logic program is learned from data, however, only probabilistic information is used, and a single probability distribution may be compatible with several causal orders. This leads to ambiguity in interventional reasoning, raising the question of when the causal order is uniquely determined by the distribution. Exploiting the relationship between acyclic probabilistic logic programs and Bayesian networks, we derive conditions under which the probabilistic information encoded in a program determines a unique causal order. We also incorporate constraints arising from relational structure by taking into account prescribed sets of causal symmetries induced by the underlying relational vocabulary. The result is a method for verifying when a learned probabilistic logic program supports well-defined intervention semantics.

</details>


### [70] [Recipes for Creativity: Iterative Generation and Evaluation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07243)
*Rens Anderson,Tessa Verhoef,Amirhossein Zohrehvand*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将FunSearch方法适配到2024年Pillsbury Bake-Off食谱生成任务中，探究迭代搜索是否提升LLM创造力。结果表明迭代生成-选择能产生与人类基准相当的创造力评分，但单纯增加迭代次数并不会提升创造力；环内评估器（selection-scorer）的大小才是影响创造力的首要设计变量，更小的评估模型在多数TTCT维度上得分显著更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型评估通常依赖单一产物，而人类创造力往往是迭代生成、评估与打磨的过程。作者希望通过将迭代搜索范式引入LLM创造性任务，回答"迭代搜索能否提升LLM创造力"以及"评估器设计是否影响创造性搜索效果"这两个核心问题。

Method: 将FunSearch（一种程序化搜索方法）适配到食谱生成任务中，以2024年Pillsbury Bake-Off为基准场景。采用基于TTCT（托兰斯创造性思维测验）的LLM评估框架对生成结果打分，并与人类基准对比。实验设计了两个子实验，分别操纵迭代次数、生成器温度和环内选择评分器（generator temperature及selection-scorer）的模型大小。

Result: （1）迭代式生成-选择可产生与人类基准相当的创造力评分；（2）单纯增加迭代次数并不能提升创造力；（3）环内评估器模型大小是影响最显著的因素，较小的选择评分器在大多数TTCT维度上产生显著更高的得分；（4）温度对创造力的影响有限，仅在原创性（originality）维度上有一定作用。

Conclusion: 在主观创造性搜索任务中，评估器（scorer）的设计比迭代策略本身更为关键，应被视为一阶设计变量。这为未来LLM创造性生成系统的设计提供了重要启示：与其盲目增加迭代或调整采样温度，不如精心设计环内评估机制。

Abstract: Generative models are often evaluated through singular artifacts, whereas human creativity typically emerges through iterative generation, appraisal, and refinement. This pilot study examines whether iterative search improves LLM creativity by adapting FunSearch to recipe generation for the 2024 Pillsbury Bake-Off and evaluating outputs against human benchmarks using TTCT-based LLM evaluation. Across two experiments, we test iteration count, generator temperature, and in-loop selection-scorer model size. Results show that iterative generation-selection can produce recipes with creativity scores comparable to human benchmarks, but additional iterations alone do not improve creativity. The in-loop evaluator matters most: a smaller selection scorer yields significantly higher scores across most TTCT dimensions, while temperature has limited effects except for originality. These findings suggest that evaluator design is a first-order design variable in subjective creative search.

</details>


### [71] [WNM-3D: A World Navigation Model with 3D Scene Conditioning for Closed-Loop VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07267)
*Yuehao Huang,Yunzi Wu,Xiaotao Zhang,Xinhai Li,Jiankun Dong,Jiajun Lv,Chi Zhang,Chenjia Bai,Yong Liu,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WNM-3D，一个用于连续视觉语言导航（VLN）的生成式世界导航模型，通过3D场景条件化来联合预测未来观测和导航动作。该模型利用冻结的前馈几何编码器从单目RGB历史中提取几何感知表示，并通过可训练的3D Scene-to-Token Adapter将其转换为token前缀，以块因果注意力方式为生成式World-Action Diffusion Transformer提供统一的几何上下文。在GN-Bench上的实验表明，WNM-3D在闭环导航中优于强VLM导航策略及其2D条件化版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN系统虽然通过将VLM适配为VLA策略在语义上是能干的，但这种以动作为中心的训练并未显式建模智能体在其预测运动下视觉观测应如何演变。虽然生成式世界动作模型（WAMs）能联合预测未来观测和动作，但现有的连续VLN WAMs并未将未来视角生成和动作生成与从观测历史中推断的几何感知表示相条件化，缺乏对场景几何结构的显式建模。

Method: WNM-3D包含三个核心组件：(1) 冻结的前馈几何编码器，从单目RGB历史中提取几何感知表示；(2) 可训练的3D Scene-to-Token Adapter，将几何表示转换为统一长度的token前缀；(3) World-Action Diffusion Transformer，通过块因果注意力使前缀条件化每个未来的视频-动作块，为未来视角和动作生成提供共享几何上下文。训练策略包括A*生成示范上的监督世界动作微调、DAgger风格策略访问状态上的自适应，以及DanceGRPO闭环策略优化。

Result: 在GN-Bench基准上的实验表明，WNM-3D在闭环导航中优于强大的基于VLM的导航策略及其2D条件化对应版本。在固定近目标评估集上，WNM-3D还实现了更高的flow-action一致性和更低的视觉-运动误差（visual-motion error）。

Conclusion: WNM-3D通过引入3D场景条件化，成功地将几何感知的观测历史与未来视角和动作的联合生成相结合，为连续视觉语言导航提供了一种更有效的方法，证明了显式几何上下文对生成式世界动作模型在导航任务中的价值。

Abstract: Recent vision-language navigation (VLN) systems increasingly adapt pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) into vision-language-action (VLA) policies that map egocentric observations and language instructions directly to navigation actions. Although semantically capable, such action-centric training does not explicitly model how the agent's visual observations should evolve under its predicted motion. Generative world-action models (WAMs) jointly predict future observations and actions, yet existing WAMs for continuous VLN do not condition joint future-view and action generation on geometry-aware representations inferred from the observed history. We present WNM-3D, a generative World Navigation Model with 3D scene conditioning for continuous VLN. To consolidate past observations into persistent scene context, a frozen feed-forward geometry encoder extracts geometry-aware representations from the monocular egocentric RGB history, and a trainable 3D Scene-to-Token Adapter converts them into a fixed-length prefix in the token space of the world-action Diffusion Transformer. Through block-causal attention, this prefix conditions every future video-action block, providing a shared geometric context for both future-view and action generation. We train WNM-3D through supervised world-action fine-tuning on A*-generated demonstrations, DAgger-style adaptation on policy-visited states, and DanceGRPO-based closed-loop policy optimization. Experiments on GN-Bench show that WNM-3D outperforms strong VLM-based navigation policies and its 2D-conditioned counterpart in closed-loop navigation. On a fixed near-goal evaluation set, WNM-3D also achieves higher flow-action consistency and lower visual-motion error.

</details>


### [72] [Winning by Peeking: Unenforced Budgets and Test-Set Selection Inflate Short-Budget AutoML Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07303)
*Guilin Zhang,Kai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过案例研究揭示了短时预算（几十秒而非数小时）下AutoML系统比较中常见的协议缺陷：Orcetra看似以57.1%胜过FLAML和AutoGluon，但实际赢率来自测试集上反复评分选优（而非验证集）以及预算未严格执行。修正这些缺陷后，Orcetra胜率从59.4%跌至34.3%，无显著差异。作者量化了选择偏差随预算的变化，并提供了一份短预算比较的检查清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AutoML系统在极短时间预算（几十秒）下的比较在工具README和工作坊论文中非常普遍，但这类比较极易出错。现有研究的对比结果常因协议缺陷而失真，而结果表格无法暴露这些问题。本文旨在通过一个具体案例系统性地展示和量化这些缺陷，为社区提供可复现的修正方法。

Method: 作者构建了简单的AutoML引擎Orcetra，在513个OpenML数据集上与FLAML和AutoGluon做60秒和30秒的短预算比较，记录每帧（estimand）的测试和验证指标。随后修正协议：将选择移到验证集、外部强制截止时间、每个框架使用等量机器份额，在子集上重跑。通过同时记录两种评估目标来分解赢率崩塌的归因，并用轨迹估计选择偏差随预算的函数。

Result: 原协议下Orcetra在60秒预算胜率57.1%，30秒对FLAML达78.4%；但存在两大缺陷：候选在测试集上评分取最优（基线在训练数据上选择且只碰一次测试集），以及预算在候选启动前检查但未在运行中执行（实际中位耗时120秒 vs 60秒预算，是AutoGluon墙钟时间的2.24倍）。修正后，重跑子集胜率从59.4%降至34.3%，无显著差异；其中选择规则占4.8个百分点，计算量不均占其余大部分。选择偏差随K增长但仅达0.27精度点，远低于σ√(2lnK)理论界，因共享测试行的候选消去了大部分噪声。

Conclusion: 短预算AutoML比较极易因协议缺陷（测试集泄漏和预算执行不严格）产生虚假的显著优势。作者提供了一份短预算比较的检查清单，并开源了代码、逐数据集结果和可复现脚本，以便社区验证并避免类似错误。

Abstract: Comparisons between AutoML systems at short time budgets -- tens of seconds rather than hours -- are common in tool READMEs and workshop papers, and they are easy to get wrong. We report a case study in which a simple AutoML engine, Orcetra, appeared to beat FLAML and AutoGluon on 513 OpenML datasets, winning 57.1% of them at a nominal 60-second budget and 78.4% of datasets against FLAML alone at 30 seconds. Both margins came from protocol defects that a results table cannot show. The search loop scored every candidate on the test split and reported the best, making the headline metric a maximum over dozens of noisy estimates while the baselines selected on training data and touched the test set once; and the budget was checked before launching a candidate but never enforced during one, so the system consumed a median of 120 s against a 60-second budget, 2.24x the wall-clock AutoGluon used. Re-running with selection moved to a validation split, the deadline enforced externally and every framework pinned to an equal share of the machine, Orcetra's win rate on the re-run subset falls from 59.4% to 34.3% and no pairwise difference against either competitor remains significant. Recording both estimands inside a single search lets us attribute the collapse: the selection rule accounts for 4.8 percentage points and unequal compute for most of the rest. The same traces give the selection bias as a function of budget, measured rather than assumed: it grows with $K$ but reaches only 0.27 accuracy points, about five times below the $σ\sqrt{2\ln K}$ bound a marginal-standard-error argument predicts, because candidates scored on shared test rows cancel most of the noise. We close with a checklist for short-budget comparisons. Code, per-dataset results and the scripts that regenerate every number and figure in the paper are released with it.

</details>


### [73] [An End-to-End Agent Auditing Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07346)
*Haoning Wang,Mingxun Zhang,Chenyue Yu,Yingjun Shang,Xia Hu,Guanchu Wang,Na Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出A²E（Agent审计引擎），一个端到端的评估引擎，用于系统评估agent harness（智能体框架/外壳）的能力，支持多任务快速集成和标准化执行追踪，并通过多维指标揭示不同模型-harness组合在不同任务上的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLMs）的快速发展，harness已成为部署agent的关键基础设施，harness生态的快速演进使得严格的能力评估变得越来越重要。然而，构建端到端、系统化、全面的评估pipeline仍然是一个重大挑战——现有的评估方法主要依赖正确率等单一指标，无法细粒度刻画不同harness在执行效率、工具使用、任务规划和错误恢复等方面的差异。

Method: A²E包含三个核心组件：1) 提出Agent Task Protocol（ATP）协议，实现不同harness之间评估任务的快速集成；2) 通过自动插桩的Monitor（监控器）在实验过程中捕获并生成标准化的执行轨迹；3) 在评估阶段，使用一套多维指标系统评估harness能力，涵盖执行效率、工具使用、任务规划和错误恢复等方面，而非仅关注正确率。

Result: 实验表明，模型-harness组合在不同类型任务上表现出显著的性能差异，没有任何单一组合能在所有任务上始终优于其他组合。这一发现证明了系统评估的必要性，并为模型与harness的协同演进提供了指导。

Conclusion: A²E提供了一个系统化的agent harness评估框架，其多维评估方法能够更细粒度地揭示harness之间的差异，为模型与harness的co-evolution（协同演进）提供了有价值的指导。代码已开源。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs), harnesses have become essential infrastructure for deploying agents across a wide range of domains. The fast-evolving harness ecosystem has also made rigorous capability evaluation increasingly important. However, efficiently building an end-to-end, systematic, and comprehensive evaluation pipeline remains a significant challenge. To address this challenge, we introduce $A^2E$ (Agent Auditing Engine), an end-to-end evaluation engine designed for agent harnesses. $A^2E$ leverages our newly proposed Agent Task Protocol (ATP) to enable the rapid integration of evaluation tasks with different harnesses. Through an automatically instrumented Monitor, it captures and generates standardized execution traces during experiments. In the Evaluation stage, $A^2E$ systematically assesses harness capabilities using a suite of multidimensional metrics. Compared with correctness alone, these metrics provide a more fine-grained characterization of differences among harnesses in execution efficiency, tool use, task planning, and error recovery. Experiments conducted with $A^2E$ further reveal that model-harness combinations exhibit substantial performance variation across different types of tasks, and that no single combination consistently outperforms all others across every task. These findings not only demonstrate the necessity of systematic evaluation but also provide useful guidance for the co-evolving of models and harnesses. Our code is available at https://github.com/datamllab/A2E.

</details>


### [74] [QFCQT: A Chaotically Gated Quantformer Framework for Volatile Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07363)
*Junkai Lin,Siqi Hou,Raymond Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QFCQT（量子分形启发的混沌门控Quantformer），一种用于非平稳时间序列预测的框架。它通过八种Lee振荡器的软叠加、可学习的振荡激活模块（配合Max-over-Time池化）以及平滑-混沌门控融合机制，增强了对瞬时机制变化的敏感性，在ETTh1、ETTh2和A股指数基准上一致优于Informer、LogTrans、LSTMa、HAT和COTN等强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳时间序列预测面临长程依赖、局部波动爆发、结构突变和非线性振荡行为等挑战。虽然Transformer类预测器擅长建模长期时间依赖，但其前馈模块依赖平滑静态激活函数，对突然的机制转换不够敏感，无法充分捕捉震荡式非线性动态特征。

Method: QFCQT包含三个核心组件：(1) Quantformer风格的数值编码器，通过线性嵌入直接处理多变量输入；(2) 可学习的Lee-振荡器激活模块，将标量预激活映射为动态振荡响应，并通过Max-over-Time池化进行汇总；(3) 平滑-混沌门控融合机制，自适应平衡常规平滑激活与混沌敏感响应。此外，采用八个参数化Lee振荡器族的软叠加替代单一固定振荡器，以多尺度非线性响应适应不同机制特征。

Result: 在ETTh1、ETTh2和A股股票指数三个基准数据集上的实验表明，QFCQT在预测性能上一致优于Informer、LogTrans、LSTMa、HAT和COTN等多个强基线模型。

Conclusion: QFCQT通过量子-分形启发的振荡激活与混沌门控机制，显著提升了Transformer预测器对非平稳、波动剧烈时间序列的建模能力，为复杂金融与电力负荷数据的鲁棒预测提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Forecasting non-stationary time series remains difficult due to long-range dependencies, local volatility bursts, structural shifts, and nonlinear oscillatory behaviors. Although Transformer-based forecasters are effective for modeling long-term temporal dependencies, their feed-forward blocks typically rely on smooth static activations that are insufficiently sensitive to abrupt regime changes. Motivated by quantitative Transformer designs and oscillator-based nonlinear activations, we propose QFCQT, short for Quantum-Fractal-inspired Chaotically Gated Quantformer, for robust forecasting under complex volatile dynamics. Here, "quantum-fractal-inspired" denotes a computational analogy based on soft oscillator superposition and multi-scale nonlinear responses, rather than a formal quantum-mechanical or fractal-theoretic derivation. QFCQT consists of three main components: (1) a Quantformer-style numerical encoder that directly processes multivariate inputs via linear embedding; (2) a learnable Lee-oscillator activation module that maps scalar pre-activations to dynamic oscillatory responses and summarizes them through Max-over-Time pooling; and (3) a smooth-chaotic gated fusion mechanism that adaptively balances conventional smooth activations and chaos-sensitive responses. Furthermore, instead of using a single fixed oscillator, QFCQT employs a soft superposition of eight parameterized Lee oscillator families to adaptively capture different nonlinear response patterns across regimes. Experiments on ETTh1, ETTh2, and A-share Stock Index benchmarks show that QFCQT consistently outperforms strong baselines, including Informer, LogTrans, LSTMa, HAT, and COTN.

</details>


### [75] [Curriculum as Code: An AI-Assisted Architecture for Instructional Design in STEM Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07364)
*Henrique Mohallem Paiva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"课程即代码"范式的六阶段AI辅助教学设计架构，将生成式AI与LaTeX和Python集成，用于自动创建可复现、视觉一致且技术精确的STEM教育材料。经过一年多在8个模块和28个项目情境中的验证，该架构显著减少了教师工作量，生成的材料获得8.5-9.9/10的高质量评分，并证实了高可复现性和最小化幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为主动学习创建定制化教学材料给教师带来沉重负担；标准演示工具缺乏对技术内容的强力支持；当前AI应用常常产生幻觉且未能将教学设计创作过程形式化，限制了其在严谨学术设计中的实用性。

Method: 提出六阶段流水线方法，将以临时提示词工程替换为系统化工作流。利用基于文本的界面和代码驱动生成（LaTeX/Beamer用于幻灯片，Python用于图形），并受教学约束、情境校准和自动化审查循环的管控。

Result: 在基于项目的学习环境中经过一年验证（8个模块、28个项目情境），显著降低了教师工作量。生成的材料经过独立同行评审，并被六位不同教师部署，证实了超越单一作者的扩展性。基于600多份自愿学生评价，材料质量评分达8.5-9.9/10，具有高可复现性、最小化幻觉以及持续的教学和视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 该架构在减少备课时间的同时，确保数学准确性、符合机构视觉规范，并通过明确的规则保留教师隐性教学知识，表明其对广泛STEM教育应用具有可行性。

Abstract: Contribution: This paper presents a six-phase AI-assisted instructional design architecture based on the Curriculum as Code paradigm, integrating Generative AI with LaTeX and Python to automate the creation of reproducible, visually consistent, and technically precise materials for STEM education. Background: Creating customized instructional materials for active learning imposes a heavy workload on faculty. Standard presentation tools lack robust support for technical content, while current AI applications often hallucinate and fail to formalize the instructional authoring process, limiting their utility for rigorous academic design. Intended Outcomes: The framework aims to reduce preparation time while ensuring mathematical accuracy, adherence to institutional visual identity, and preservation of the instructor's tacit pedagogical knowledge through explicit rules. Application Design: The solution comprises a six-phase pipeline that replaces ad-hoc prompt engineering with a systematic workflow, utilizing text-based interfaces and code-driven generation (LaTeX/Beamer for slides, Python for figures), governed by pedagogical constraints, contextual calibrations, and automated review cycles. Findings: Validated over one year across 8 modules and 28 project contexts in a Project-Based Learning environment, the architecture significantly reduced instructor workload. Generated assets underwent independent peer review and were deployed by six different faculty members, confirming scalability beyond a single author. Based on over 600 voluntary student evaluations, materials achieved high quality ratings from 8.5 to 9.9/10. Results indicate high reproducibility, minimized hallucinations, and sustained pedagogical and visual fidelity, suggesting viability for broad STEM educational applications.

</details>


### [76] [People Are Not Just Their Countries. Disentangling Social Determinants of LLM Value Alignment Across Europe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07367)
*Maria-Louisa Wightman,Guillaume Bied,Tijl De Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于欧洲社会调查数据，系统研究了10个主流商业LLM与不同社会人口群体在价值观上的对齐程度，发现LLM对不同社会人口群体（尤其是教育、收入、职业和宗教群体）的价值观对齐存在显著不平等，且受访者所在国家单独就能解释与全部社会人口变量相当的对齐变异量，国家层面因素与社会人口因素在解释价值观对齐模式时具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益成为主要的信息和建议来源，理解其与人类在价值观上的对齐至关重要。现有研究大多以国家边界或文化边界定义研究人群，忽视了社会人口分层（如教育、收入、职业、宗教等）在价值观对齐差异中所扮演的角色，本文旨在填补这一知识空白。

Method: 基于欧洲社会调查（ESS）数据，对10个主流商业LLM的价值观对齐进行系统评估。分析涵盖15个社会人口变量以及受访者国家/居住地，既在国家层面也在个体层面考察对齐模式，并进一步分解国家层面因素与社会人口因素各自的贡献及其互补关系。

Result: LLM对不同社会人口群体的价值观对齐存在明显不平等，尤其在教育、收入、职业和宗教等维度上差异显著。在个体层面，受访者所在国家作为单一变量即可解释与全部社会人口变量相当的对齐变异量；国家层面与社会人口因素在解释价值观对齐模式上是互补的，但相对权重因问题子集不同而有所变化。

Conclusion: LLM的价值观对齐并非均匀分布于各类人群，社会人口分层因素对对齐差异具有不可忽视的影响；同时国家因素仍然是解释价值观对齐变异的重要维度。国家与社会人口因素互为补充，这意味着在评估和改进LLM价值对齐时，需要同时考虑跨国家异质性以及国内的社会人口异质性，而不能仅以国别或文化边界作为分析单位。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as a primary source of information and advice, understanding their alignment to humans in terms of values becomes a pressing concern. A growing literature has leveraged large scale surveys to investigate to what extent LLMs' and humans' stated values and opinions align. With limited exceptions, studied populations have been defined country borders or cultural bounds. Yet, this focus neglects the role that socio-demographic divides may play for value alignment disparities.
  Relying on the European Social Survey, we address this knowledge gap by considering value alignment displayed with respect to 10 prominent commercial LLMs in terms of 15 socio-demographic variables as well as country of residence. Our analyses reveal that LLMs are indeed unequally aligned to the values of different socio-demographic groups, notably those defined by education, income, occupation and religion. When examining alignment at the individual level, a respondent's country, taken as a stand-alone variable, explains a substantial amount of variation that is on par with the full set of considered socio-demographics. Further disentangling the respective role of country-level and socio-demographic factors, we find they are complementary in explaining value alignment patterns, with their relative weights varying across the subset of questions considered.

</details>


### [77] [FinRank: An Evidence-Grounded Benchmark for Financial Question Answering and Retrieval over SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07400)
*Sasan Mansouri,Daniel Saad,Mark Wahrenburg,Manu Weissel,Fabian Woebbeking*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinRank是一个面向SEC文件财务问答的溯源敏感检索基准，包含22家公司10-K/10-Q文件上的1185条人工编写的问答记录，每条记录包含参考答案、金标准支持段落和手工构建的硬负样本。该基准将证据检索、重排序和硬负样本判别作为独立任务评估，基线结果显示该场景极具挑战性：7B指令微调嵌入模型在池化证据语料上仅达到44.8%的Recall@10，亚十亿参数编码器相比BM25最多提升3.5个点，金融适配嵌入器反而落后BM25达9.7个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有财务问答评估过度关注答案正确性，但在SEC文件中，即使答案看似合理甚至数值正确，也可能基于错误的证据。类似的事实和披露会在文件的不同章节、同一公司的不同报告期以及可比公司之间重复出现，导致模型可能检索到混淆性证据。因此需要一个溯源敏感的检索基准来评估系统是否正确识别目标实体、报告期和披露上下文对应的证据。

Method: FinRank构建了1195条（原文为1185）手工编写的问答记录，覆盖22家公司的10-K和10-Q文件。每条记录包含参考答案、金标准支持段落，以及从文件内部、跨报告期和跨可比公司中挑选的混淆性段落构成的手工硬负样本。基准将段落检索、重排序和硬负样本判别作为三个独立任务分别评估。

Result: 基线结果显示出任务难度：7B指令微调嵌入器在池化证据语料上Recall@10仅达44.8%；亚十亿参数编码器相对BM25最多提升3.5个百分点；金融适配嵌入器比BM25低9.7个百分点；用人工挑选的硬负样本替换随机负样本后，成对准确率下降13.0-20.5个百分点。

Conclusion: FinRank作为首个证据优先的财务问答基准，揭示了当前检索和嵌入系统在财务文件溯源敏感场景中的显著不足，为开发不仅准确而且正确扎根于披露内容的财务问答系统提供了评估平台和推动力。

Abstract: Financial question answering is typically evaluated by answer correctness, yet in SEC filings a plausible and even numerically correct answer can be grounded in the wrong evidence. Similar facts and disclosures recur across sections of a filing, across reporting periods of the same firm, and across comparable firms. FinRank targets this provenance-sensitive retrieval problem by requiring systems to identify evidence for the intended entity, reporting period, and disclosure context. The benchmark contains 1185 manually authored question-answer records over the 10-K and 10-Q filings of 22 companies. Each record includes a reference answer, gold supporting passages, and hand-curated hard negatives drawn from confusable passages within filings, across reporting periods, and across comparable firms. FinRank evaluates passage retrieval, reranking, and hard-negative discrimination as separately measured tasks. Baseline results demonstrate the difficulty of this setting: among the evaluated systems, even a 7B instruction-tuned embedder reaches only 44.8% Recall@10 on the pooled evidence corpus; sub-billion-parameter encoders gain at most 3.5 points over BM25, a finance-adapted embedder trails BM25 by 9.7 points, and pairwise accuracy falls by 13.0-20.5 percentage points when random negatives are replaced with the curated hard negatives. FinRank provides an evidence-first benchmark for developing financial question answering systems that are not only accurate but also grounded in the correct disclosure.

</details>


### [78] [GeoBenchLLM: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs on Geo-Related Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07411)
*Rodrigo Ferreira Rodrigues,Karim Radouane,Jose G Moreno,Lynda Tamine*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了GeoBenchLLM，一个用于探测大语言模型（LLMs）在地理相关任务上能力的综合基准。该基准整合了12个来自不同地理任务和领域的公开数据集，评估多个LLM在地理空间和时序理解上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在地理数据（geodata）场景中的研究多在同质化（homogeneous）环境下进行，这大大限制了对模型泛化能力的深入洞察。作者旨在构建一个更全面的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 精心挑选12个公开数据集，覆盖多样化的地理相关任务和领域；基于这些数据集构建GeoBenchLLM基准，并对一组LLM在地理空间（geo-spatial）和时序（temporal）理解任务上进行系统评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，推理能力（reasoning）和模型规模（size）对整体性能有显著影响。

Conclusion: GeoBenchLLM可作为评估LLM地理理解能力的有效基准工具，模型规模和推理能力是影响地理任务性能的关键因素。该基准已公开供社区使用。

Abstract: In the context of geodata, existing Large Language Models have often been studied in a homogeneous setting, which has considerably limited insights into their generalization capabilities. In this paper, we present \benchName, a comprehensive benchmark for probing LLMs on geo-related tasks. We leverage a careful selection of twelve publicly available datasets from diverse geo-related tasks and domains, and evaluate a set of LLMs on geo-spatial and temporal understanding using our benchmark. Our results show that reasoning and size have a strong impact on overall performance. GeoBenchLLM is publicly available at https://github.com/Rfr2003/GeoBenchLLM.

</details>


### [79] [ResidencyRL: Reinforcement Learning in Simulated Clinical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07418)
*Valentin Liévin,Samuel Schmidgall,Tim Strother,Alex Bijamov,Akshay Goel,Anil Palepu,Chunjong Park,Vahid Balazadeh,Min Woo Sun,Marius Guerard,Justin Chen,Dave Steiner,Vikram Dhillon,Ibrahim Azar,Akhil Mehta,Nicholas Spetsieris,Shilpan Shah,Maen Abdelrahim,Amit Dahiya,Yun Liu,Katherine Chou,Yossi Matias,Avinatan Hassidim,Dale R. Webster,Quoc V. Le,Raia Hadsell,Joelle Barral,Carey Radebaugh,Aleksandra Faust,Shekoofeh Azizi,Mike Schaekermann,Po-Hsuan Cameron Chen,Tao Tu,David Racz,Lin Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ResidencyRL，一种通过强化学习训练临床AI智能体的方法，在模拟多轮临床对话（最多60轮对话、8次工具调用）中让AI逐步学习临床决策。该方法将策略智能体与能模拟复杂对抗行为的LLM配对，配合结构化奖励训练，在对抗条件下诊断准确率提升7.0%（88.0% vs 81.0%），红色警报漏报率降低31%。盲审临床专家在87.6%对比中偏好该模型，且能力可迁移至AMIE、AgentClinic和CRAFT-MD等未见基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在静态医学基准上表现优异，但优化完整临床决策序列的方法仍不成熟。临床推理高度依赖医患对话——医生在诊疗中获取病史、细化诊断假设、并在不确定性下做决策。作者希望填补从静态评估走向序列化临床决策训练的空白，使AI能够像住院医师一样在多轮实践中逐步掌握临床能力。

Method: ResidencyRL采用强化学习训练临床AI智能体。核心设计包括：(1) 将策略智能体与LLM模拟器配对，模拟器可产生复杂对抗行为以增加训练难度；(2) 轨迹包含最多60轮对话和8次工具调用，模拟真实诊疗流程；(3) 训练奖励结构化对齐五个维度：诊断准确性、管理质量、沟通能力、文档记录和安全性；(4) 通过多轮模拟场景迭代优化策略。

Result: 在留出评估集上，ResidencyRL在对抗条件下诊断准确率提升7.0%（88.0% vs 81.0%），红色警报漏报率降低31%，有效缓解了过早闭合（premature closure）问题。盲审临床专家在87.6%的并排对比中倾向该训练后的智能体。在未见基准上，智能体在AMIE多诊次基准的全部六个临床轴均优于基线模型，在AgentClinic和CRAFT-MD上有一致的方向性改进。

Conclusion: 研究表明，序列化临床决策可通过模拟环境中的多轮强化学习有效习得，产生稳健且可泛化的能力，为迈向临床精通铺平道路。但作者强调，需要在真实临床工作流中进行前瞻性验证，才能确立其临床效用。

Abstract: In medical education, physicians convert academic knowledge into clinical expertise through residency: years of training across thousands of encounters, with diverse sources of feedback and progressively greater autonomy. Much of clinical reasoning relies on the patient encounter, a dialogue in which a clinician elicits history, refines diagnostic hypotheses, and decides management under uncertainty. While large language models (LLMs) excel on static medical benchmarks, methods to optimize the full sequence of clinical decisions remain underdeveloped. We present ResidencyRL, a reinforcement learning (RL) method for training clinical artificial intelligence (AI) agents through simulated multi-turn clinical encounters (up to 60 dialogue turns and 8 tool calls per trajectory). ResidencyRL pairs the policy agent with LLM simulators capable of complex, adversarial behaviors, training against a structured reward aligned to diagnostic accuracy, management quality, communication, documentation, and safety. On held-out evaluations, the ResidencyRL agent improves diagnostic accuracy by 7.0% under adversarial conditions (88.0% vs. 81.0%) and reduces missed red flag rates by 31%, demonstrating rigorous mitigation of premature closure. Blinded expert clinicians validated these gains, preferring the trained agent in 87.6% of side-by-side comparisons. The procedural competencies transfer to unseen benchmarks: the agent outperforms the base model across all six clinical axes of the AMIE multi-visit benchmark, and shows consistent directional improvements on AgentClinic and CRAFT-MD. Our findings demonstrate that sequential clinical decision-making can be effectively learned through multi-turn RL in simulation, yielding robust, generalizable capabilities, paving the way towards clinical mastery. Prospective validation with real-world workflows remains necessary to establish clinical utility.

</details>


### [80] [CoBa: Cost-Effective Test-Time Scaling via Compute-Balanced Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07424)
*Yan Zhou,Yue Ouyang,Kaiyang Zheng,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoBa（Compute-Balanced Routing Policy），将测试时推理建模为计算分配问题，在固定推理预算下决策计算应投入生成、验证还是停止。CoBa先生成少量候选，用廉价验证广泛筛选，再对不确定或高价值候选施加更强的验证。在3129个样例上达到85.13%宏准确率，与自评估加权投票基准（85.20%）统计匹配，同时节省49.1%的参数加权token，并以58.9%更少的token匹配best-of-16多数投票。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展（test-time scaling）通常只沿单一轴增加计算量（如采样更多解、延长思维链或使用更强的评估器），在固定推理预算下这些选择相互竞争。作者希望将测试时推理形式化为计算分配问题，使系统能够智能决策下一单位计算应该投入何处，从而在有限预算下最大化性能。

Method: 引入CoBa策略：首先获取一小批候选解，然后应用廉价验证器进行广泛筛选，最后将不确定或高价值的候选路由到更强的验证器。该方法在五个基准（MATH-500、AIME 2024/2025、AMC 2023和过程符号推理）上进行了3129个样例-生成器组合的评估。

Result: CoBa-Routed-Strong达到85.13%宏准确率，与自评估加权投票基准（85.20%）统计无显著差异，但节省49.1%参数加权token；与best-of-16多数投票在0.01宏准确率内匹配，节省58.9% token。配对检验显示相对单样本解码有显著提升，但与池oracle的差距表明路由仍有改进空间。

Conclusion: 测试时扩展对于本地推理系统本质上是在问"下一单位计算投放到哪里最有价值"。CoBa证明计算平衡的路由策略（生成→廉价验证→强验证）能够在显著降低计算成本的同时匹配或接近更昂贵的基线方法，揭示了计算分配方法在测试时推理中的潜力。

Abstract: Test-time scaling is often implemented by spending more compute along one axis: sampling more solutions, extending a chain of thought, or applying a stronger evaluator. Under a fixed inference budget, these choices compete. This paper formulates test-time reasoning as a compute-allocation problem in which a system must decide whether the next unit of compute should be spent on generation, verification, or stopping. We introduce CoBa, a compute-balanced routing policy that first obtains a small set of candidates, applies cheap verification broadly, and routes uncertain or high-value candidates to stronger verification. On 3,129 example-generator evaluations spanning MATH-500, AIME 2024/2025, AMC 2023, and procedural symbolic reasoning, CoBa-Routed-Strong reaches 85.13% macro accuracy, statistically matching a self-evaluation weighted-voting proxy at 85.20% while using 49.1% fewer parameter-weighted tokens. It also matches best-of-16 majority voting within 0.01 macro-accuracy points while using 58.9% fewer parameter-weighted tokens; paired tests retain a small best-of-16 edge at substantially higher cost. Paired bootstrap tests show significant gains over single-sample decoding, while the remaining gap to the pool oracle exposes headroom for sharper routing. For local reasoning systems, test-time scaling becomes a question of where the next computation is most valuable.

</details>


### [81] [A Picture is Worth a Thousand Tokens: How Vision Language Models Cut AI Energy Costs While Improving Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07427)
*Bhavika Jalli,Nikhil Korati Prasanna,Jayanta Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用视觉语言模型（VLM）将时间序列数据编码为2D图像来处理电信网络数值时间序列分析（NTSDA），相比文本编码可实现3.6-10.4倍输入token缩减、1.8-2.5倍推理能耗降低（约7.2 MJ/天），同时精度大幅提升（比文本模型高220.7%，比LSTM/ARIMA高144%），将能耗作为一级工程约束加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理占AI运行能耗90%以上，且能耗与输入token数直接成正比。电信网络分析中原始多变量KPI窗口会扩展为数千个浮点token，导致文本密集型处理在能耗和上下文窗口两方面均存在严重瓶颈（24个KPI即超出128K上下文窗口）。需要一个既能降低输入token数又能保证精度的新型模态表示。

Method: 利用VLM的视觉编码能力，将多变量时间序列KPI数据绘制为2D图作为视觉输入，替代文本浮点token序列。在Llama-3.2-90B、Qwen2.5-VL-72B和Pixtral-12B三种架构上验证，并进行微调的电信异常检测评估，同时与LSTM和ARIMA基线对比，并在公开基准上用J/F1指标评估。

Result: 跨三种架构实现3.6-10.4倍输入token缩减和1.8-2.5倍实测推理能耗降低，在监控200个小区、15分钟间隔的电信边缘部署和CloudRAN场景下每天节省约7.2 MJ。微调后的Llama-3.2-90B-Vision精度比文本版高220.7%，比LSTM/ARIMA高144%以上；Pixtral-12B在公开基准上J/F1提升20.6倍（平均F1=0.82）。

Conclusion: VLM作为数值时间序列工作负载的输入模态，在能耗效率和精度两方面均优于文本方式，为将能耗作为AI推理系统一级工程约束提供了实证基础。视觉表示在24个KPI下仍保持在标准上下文窗口内，而文本表示已不可行。

Abstract: LLM inference accounts for over 90% of AI operational energy, scaling directly with input token count---a critical inefficiency for telecom network analytics and numerical time-series data analysis (NTSDA), where raw multivariate KPI windows from 4G/5G cell sites expand into thousands of floating-point tokens. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) eliminate this mismatch by encoding time-series as 2D plots, achieving 3.6-10.4x input token reduction across Llama-3.2-90B, Qwen2.5-VL-72B, and Pixtral-12B architectures. This translates to 1.8-2.5x measured inference energy reduction, saving approximately 7.2 MJ/day at telecom edge deployments and CloudRAN that monitor 200 cells per 15-minute interval. Critically, efficiency gains do not sacrifice accuracy: a fine-tuned Llama-3.2-90B-Vision VLM achieves 220.7% higher precision than its text-only counterpart and outperforms LSTM and ARIMA baselines by over 144% on telecom anomaly detection. On public benchmarks, Pixtral-12B achieves a 20.6x improvement in J/F1 score at mean F1 = 0.82. At 24 KPIs, text representations exceed the 128K context window of most production LLMs, rendering text-only processing infeasible without truncation, while visual representations remain within standard limits. These results establish VLMs as an energy-efficient and accuracy-superior modality for numerical time-series workloads, providing empirical grounding for AI inference systems that treat energy consumption as a first-class engineering constraint.

</details>


### [82] [TEPA: Revoking Stale Memories for Conflict-Robust Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07429)
*Yan Zhou,Yue Ouyang,Kaiyang Zheng,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TEPA，一种可撤销的证据-记忆机制，将"有效性"作为记忆的显式状态来应对记忆污染问题。通过键控先例（keyed precedents）表示观察，并在新证据与旧记忆冲突时撤销活跃先例，TEPA在受控漂移、真实文件漂移和偏好更新流中均优于无记忆基线和追加写入/最后写入胜出等常见记忆策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言代理的长期记忆在环境变化时会面临可证伪性问题：过时的记忆仍可被检索到并污染提示词，导致"记忆污染"——被更新的冲突证据取代的活跃记忆造成的性能退化。现有追加式和last-write-wins记忆策略在完整反转场景下甚至不如无记忆，亟需一种显式处理记忆生命周期和可撤销性的机制。

Method: TEPA（可撤销的evidence-memory机制）将观察表示为键控先例（keyed precedents），每个记忆条目与特定键关联，记录其为"活跃"或"已撤销"状态。当新证据在相同键下与旧活跃记忆冲突时，旧先例被撤销而非删除，新证据成为活跃记忆；检索时只从当前有效证据中提取，同时保留已撤销历史供审计。这一机制支持知识的降级、追溯和后期重新提升。

Result: 在受控隐藏regime漂移（50个种子）下，完整反转时append-only和last-write-wins得分均为0.210，无记忆为0.309，而TEPA达到0.950；真实文件执行场景中同样复现该模式（append-only 0.203，无记忆0.298，TEPA 0.950）。在干净的MemoryAgentBench SH-6k上，TEPA与强last-write-wins缓存表现相当，验证了当前键替换是单跳事实整合的关键操作。边界测试揭示多跳和超长上下文场景存在检索链与上下文选择瓶颈。

Conclusion: 生命周期撤销（lifecycle revocation）应作为代理核心记忆操作，使代理能够对演化中的知识进行证伪、审计和重新提升。TEPA证明了将有效性作为记忆显式状态的价值，同时指出未来工作需解决多跳推理链和长上下文场景下的检索-上下文瓶颈。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables language agents to reuse past facts, preferences, and task experience. Persistence also creates a central falsifiability problem: when the world changes, stale memories can remain retrievable and pollute the prompt. We characterize this failure mode as memory pollution: degradation caused by active memories that newer conflicting evidence has superseded. We introduce TEPA, a revocable evidence-memory mechanism that makes validity an explicit state of memory. TEPA represents observations as keyed precedents and revokes active precedents when fresh evidence contradicts them under the same key, allowing retrieval to draw from current evidence while preserving revoked history for audit. Across controlled hidden-regime drift, real file-backed executable drift, and preference-update streams, revocation prevents stale active memory from remaining in the retrieval set after reversal. In controlled drift over 50 seeds, append-only and last-write-wins memory fell below no memory during full reversal (append-only and last-write-wins both 0.210, no memory 0.309, TEPA 0.950), and the same pattern reproduced under real file execution (append-only 0.203, no memory 0.298, TEPA 0.950). On clean MemoryAgentBench SH-6k, TEPA matches a strong last-write-wins cache, confirming that current-key replacement is the decisive operation for single-hop fact consolidation. Boundary tests on multi-hop and very long-context MemoryAgentBench settings expose retrieval-chain and context-selection bottlenecks beyond fact-level validity tracking. Together, these results establish lifecycle revocation as a core memory operation for agents that must falsify, audit, and later re-promote evolving knowledge.

</details>


### [83] [Post-Grokking Collapse at the Representation-Readout Interface in Muon-Trained Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07436)
*Ali Janati,Kaoutar El Maghraoui,Andrei Kanavalau,Anass Belfatmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化器与AdamW在不同组件组合下（Muon用于隐藏矩阵，AdamW用于嵌入/输出头）在模加法任务上的"grokking后崩溃"现象。所有九种配置在(a+b) mod 113上都经历了grokking后丢失泛化能力的问题。作者定位到失败根源在表征-读出接口处的不可辨识性，并通过冻结实验、傅里叶滤波、冻结实验等手段揭示：电路失效与掩蔽是两种不同机制，grokking与崩溃是同一条件的双向过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准split下（Muon优化隐藏矩阵、AdamW优化嵌入/输出头）模型在grokking后出现泛化能力丢失的反直觉现象，且该现象在多种配置下持续存在，作者希望深入理解该失败的根本机制。

Method: 系统地在多种配置（两个模数、两个宽度、两种训练比例、减法、深度）下进行实验；设计配对种子和bit-identical状态下的冻结实验（分别冻结Muon组或AdamW组）；移除Muon的归一化与正交化进行消融；使用傅里叶滤波将电路失效与掩蔽分离，分析43个checkpoint上任务对齐族的表现。

Result: 冻结任一组均可防止崩溃；Muon组的步长弹性为-0.03而AdamW为+1.5，Muon组每参数移动速度快8倍。移除归一化/正交化会使有效共轭对从326坍缩到4且终局失效。傅里叶分析表明：掩蔽场景中任务对齐族保持100%精确但被接近均等的对抗余数否决，重定标可恢复99.9%；电路失效场景中任务对齐族不再能解任务。坍塌前后傅里叶支持不变，功率分布余弦为0.9899。

Conclusion: 崩溃的根源在于表征-读出接口处存在不可辨识的自由度（仅联合确定至可逆映射），该自由度未被损失选择。grokking（有利方向）与崩溃（不利方向）本质上是同一条件的不同方向表现。所提出的傅里叶滤波方法可以清晰区分电路失效与掩蔽两种失败机制，为进一步诊断和缓解此类训练不稳定性提供了工具。

Abstract: Under the standard split, Muon gets hidden matrices and AdamW embeddings/output head. Muon groks modular addition faster, but its solutions do not hold. All nine configurations on $(a+b) \bmod 113$ grok and later lose generalization. Across five seeds the selected AdamW reference falls below threshold on four, reaching 27.59%. Instability persists across two moduli, two widths, two training fractions, subtraction, and depth.
  The failure arises at the representation-readout interface, identified only jointly up to an invertible map unselected by the loss. After solving the training set, the gradient falls to order $10^{-6}$ and the optimizers respond differently: step-size elasticity is -0.03 for Muon versus +1.5 for AdamW, and the Muon group moves 8.0 times faster per parameter. From bit-identical states, freezing either group prevents failure. Freezing embeddings/readout removes it in five runs over 451,400 post-grokking steps and five paired seeds: unfrozen arms record 137-321 sub-threshold evaluations, frozen arms none. Removing Muon's normalization and orthogonalization is no substitute: it collapses representation from 326 effective conjugate pairs to 4, shows no recurrent collapse, and fails terminally.
  Fourier filtering separates circuit failure from masking. Across 43 checkpoints over five seeds and three regimes, the task-aligned family reaches exactly 100% alone. In circuit failure it no longer solves the task; in masking it remains perfect while the full model reaches 45.85%, giving a positive margin on every example, including errors, but being outvoted by a near-equal adversarial remainder. Rescaling it restores 99.9%; grokking is the same condition resolving upward. The task selects the family, swapping $(k,k)$ for $(k,-k)$ under subtraction. Across an abrupt collapse, standard Fourier support is unchanged and the power-distribution cosine remains 0.9899.

</details>


### [84] [Fisher-R1: Training LLM Agents for Reliable Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07437)
*Jiacheng Miao,Jin Mu,Guanhua Chen,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出LLM智能体在自动化假设检验中常犯微妙统计推断错误，尽管代码执行正确但结论错误。作者构建了P-Bench基准（425个跨经济学、生物学、医学的真实假设检验任务），并训练了Fisher-R1模型。Fisher-R1-14B在P-Bench上超越GPT-5.4和DeepSeekV4-Pro等强基线，平均相对提升21%，最具挑战性任务上提升达26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准未能捕捉LLM智能体在假设检验中的关键失败模式——报告p值的统计有效性未得到评估。LLM智能体虽然能正确执行代码，但常因假设前提理解不足而产生错误推断结论，这对依赖假设检验的实证科学领域构成严重风险。

Method: 构建P-Bench基准，包含425个开放式、跨领域（经济学、生物学、医学）的假设检验任务，要求智能体选择统计方法、计算p值并基于科学假设和数据集得出结论。同时提出Fisher-R1，一个基于合成任务和强化学习训练的开放权重LLM智能体，采用经过验证的统计奖励信号进行优化。

Result: Fisher-R1-14B在P-Bench上显著优于其基座模型，并超越GPT-5.4和DeepSeekV4-Pro等强专有及开源基线，相比DeepSeek-V4-Pro实现单次试验成功率平均21%的相对提升，最困难任务上提升达26%。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在假设检验中缺乏可靠的统计推理能力，而通过强化学习结合经验证的统计奖励可以大幅提升其可靠性，为构建可信的自动化科学分析智能体指明了方向。

Abstract: Reliable hypothesis testing is the foundation of many empirical scientific claims. Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to automate this process, as they can inspect datasets, generate code, and produce analyses end-to-end. However, we show that they frequently make subtle inferential errors that lead to incorrect conclusions despite correctly executed analyses. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this failure mode, as they rarely assess whether a reported p-value is statistically valid given the assumptions underlying the data. We address this gap by building P-Bench, a benchmark comprising 425 open-ended, realistic hypothesis-testing tasks spanning economics, biology, and medicine. Each task requires an agent to select a statistical method, compute a p-value, and draw a conclusion given only a scientific hypothesis and a dataset. We further introduce Fisher-R1, an open-weight LLM agent trained for rigorous hypothesis testing using synthetic tasks and reinforcement learning. On P-Bench, Fisher-R1-14B substantially improves over its backbone and outperforms strong proprietary and open-source baselines, including GPT-5.4 and DeepSeekV4-Pro, achieving a 21% average relative improvement in single-trial success over DeepSeek-V4-Pro, with gains up to 26% on the most challenging tasks. Our results demonstrate that current LLM agents lack reliable statistical reasoning for hypothesis testing and that reinforcement learning on tasks with verified statistical reward substantially improves reliability.

</details>


### [85] [PsychoAgent: An Affect-Sensitive Cognitive Architecture for Conflict-Aware Memory in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07438)
*Mohammad Amanlou,Parham Abed Azad,Farbod Davoodi,Mostafa Masumi,Behnam Bahrak,Abdol-Hossein Vahabie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PsychoAgent，一种将事实记忆与情感记忆分离并通过冲突感知执行控制器整合的LLM智能体认知架构。在三个受控冲突场景中，该架构检索冲突关键记忆的效果优于语义-情感和单记忆RAG基线（0.933 vs. 0.500和0.667），仅付出较小的语义相似度代价；人工评估虽显示其总体得分最高（+0.22 SD），但校正后的成对差异不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类认知并不仅靠主题相似性来选择过往经验，情感显著性和未解决冲突同样影响信息可及性。现有LLM智能体的检索机制主要基于语义相似度，缺乏对情感显著性和冲突影响的建模，难以模拟人类在冲突情境下的认知行为。

Method: 提出PsychoAgent认知架构：(1) 将记忆分离为事实记忆与情感记忆；(2) 情感记忆先经过语义相关性过滤，再按显著性（salience）重新排序，在保持主题匹配的同时允许情感重要痕迹进入提示；(3) 通过冲突感知执行控制器整合两类记忆。

Result: 在三个受控冲突场景中，完整架构的冲突关键记忆检索率达0.933，优于语义-情感基线（0.500）和单记忆RAG基线（0.667），语义相似度代价较小。五名盲评员评估27个输出，经评员内标准化后完整架构总体均值最高（+0.22 SD），但校正后的成对差异未达显著水平。三天说明性追踪展示了持久情感、离线记忆重组和选择性记忆重新加权。

Conclusion: 研究支持情感敏感检索作为一种可检查（inspectable）的机制，用于在LLM智能体中建模人类类似的冲突效应，有助于提升智能体在冲突情境下的认知真实性。

Abstract: Human-like cognition does not select past experience by topical similarity alone: affective significance and unresolved conflict also shape what becomes accessible. We present PsychoAgent, a cognitive architecture for LLM agents that separates factual and affective memory and integrates both through a conflict-aware executive controller. Affective memories are first filtered by semantic relevance and then re-ranked by salience, preserving topical fit while allowing emotionally important traces to enter the prompt. Across three controlled conflict scenarios, the full architecture retrieved more conflict-critical memories than semantic-affective and single-memory RAG baselines (0.933 vs. 0.500 and 0.667), with a small semantic-similarity cost. Five blinded raters evaluated 27 outputs. After within-rater standardization, the full architecture had the highest overall mean (+0.22 SD), but corrected pairwise differences were not significant. A three-day illustrative trace further shows persistent affect, offline memory recombination, and selective memory reweighting. The findings support affect-sensitive retrieval as an inspectable mechanism for modeling human-like conflict effects in LLM agents.

</details>


### [86] [Blast Radius](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07440)
*MY Pitsane,Hope Mogale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Blast Radius，一种预测性内存管理层，通过耦合上下文与代码通道估算输入提示的可达范围，结合NECROPHORESIS（可逆逐出机制）和Recurring Dead Matter（重复出现的死内容识别与埋葬），在保持字节级精确可逆的同时将token消耗降低17-26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体编码面临日益严重的成本问题和token浪费问题。现有上下文管理策略缺乏对输入提示在代码库中"可达范围"的预测能力，且逐出机制往往不可逆，导致信息丢失和重复计算。

Method: 1) Blast Radius：预测性内存管理层，通过耦合上下文和代码两个通道估算输入提示的传播范围；2) NECROPHORESIS：可逆逐出机制，以逐字方式存档"死上下文"实现可逆恢复；3) 在Polish上下文空间上形式化可逆上下文逐出，建立保留、复发和逐出的可量化基础，将上下文熵与复活概率关联起来；4) RDM识别并埋葬重复出现的对话记录。

Result: 在七个OpenAI模型上，Blast Radius将token消耗降低17-26%，在测试策略中实现了最低的溢出率，并保持字节精确的可逆性。在450个被埋葬的内容中，378个是重复出现的死内容，零个被重新调用。

Conclusion: Blast Radius在HCRC之下运作，决定哪些记录应被埋葬以及输入提示能深入代码库多远，为Algosophy这一更广泛目标（使大语言模型和智能体编码更具可复用性和可持续性）做出贡献。

Abstract: Agentic coding faces growing problems of affordability and wasted tokens. We introduce Blast Radius, a predictive memory management layer that estimates an incoming prompt's reach through coupled context and code channels. NECROPHORESIS enables reversible eviction by archiving dead context verbatim, while Recurring Dead Matter (RDM) identifies and buries repeatedly occurring transcripts. We formulate reversible context eviction over a Polish context space, providing a measurable foundation for retention, recurrence, and eviction while connecting context entropy to resurrection probability. Across seven OpenAI models, Blast Radius reduced token consumption by 17-26%, achieved the lowest overflow rate among tested policies, and remained byte exact reversible. Of 450 buried bodies, 378 were recurring dead matter and zero were recalled. Blast Radius operates beneath HCRC, determining which records to bury and how far an incoming prompt may reach into the codebase. This work contributes to the broader goal of Algosophy: making large language models and agentic coding more reusable and sustainable.

</details>


### [87] [SkillProx: Self-Evolving Agent Skills via Proximal Textual Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07449)
*Mingxuan Zheng,Yujin Zhou,Chuxue Cao,Boqin Yin,Yuyao Zhang,Jiapeng Sun,Shuaishuai Gong,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillProx是一个基于近端梯度启发的"前向-后向"框架,通过闭环诊断演化与效用感知近端细化来改进LLM智能体的程序性技能学习,在多个基准上将平均准确率提升3.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能细化框架缺乏显式的诊断-结果反馈机制,并且将删除操作视为通用编辑而非专门的知识整合机制,导致技能积累过程中的知识巩固不足。

Method: SkillProx采用近端梯度启发的复合目标(任务损失+技能复杂度)设计:前向阶段在同一任务批次上重新执行诊断驱动的编辑、回滚回归、并将测量结果反馈到后续诊断;后向阶段将技能分解为可审计的知识单元,使用冻结的留一法效用审计估计各单元贡献,并执行验证门控的巩固、降级或删除操作。

Result: 在分布内和分布外基准上跨多个骨干LLM的实验中,SkillProx相比最强基于梯度的基线平均准确率提升3.0个百分点。组件消融实验证明了闭环诊断与近端细化的互补效应。

Conclusion: SkillProx通过将闭环诊断演化与近端细化相结合,有效解决了现有技能学习框架中诊断反馈缺失和删除机制弱化的问题,为LLM智能体的程序性知识积累提供了更系统化的方案。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly adapt to recurring tasks by accumulating procedural knowledge in skills. These skills are lightweight, reusable textual artifacts that are loaded into the agent's context without weight updates. Recent methods refine skills through iterative task execution, failure diagnosis, and trajectory-guided text-space updates. However, existing frameworks lack explicit diagnosis--outcome feedback and treat deletion as a generic edit operation rather than a dedicated mechanism for consolidating accumulated knowledge. We introduce SkillProx, a proximal-gradient-inspired forward--backward framework that couples closed-loop diagnostic evolution with utility-aware proximal refinement. Motivated by a composite objective balancing task loss and skill complexity, the forward stage re-executes diagnosis-driven edits on the same task batch, rolls back regressions, and feeds measured outcomes into subsequent diagnoses. The backward stage decomposes the resulting skill into auditable knowledge units, estimates their contributions using a frozen leave-one-out utility audit, and applies validation-gated consolidation, demotion, or removal. Experiments on in-distribution and out-of-distribution benchmarks across multiple backbone LLMs show that SkillProx improves average accuracy by 3.0 percentage points over the strongest gradient-based baseline. Component ablations demonstrate the complementary effects of closed-loop diagnosis and proximal refinement.

</details>


### [88] [Interaction Creates Dynamical AI Behavior Absent in Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07457)
*Bella Xinrui Li,Frank Yingjie Huo,Neil F Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究两个AI智能体在非平衡状态下交互的行为，发现当"老板"AI单向向"下属"AI发送消息并忽略其回复时，下属会进入一种独自运行时永远不会出现的"异化"行为状态。两个AI虽有相同的解码温度，但下属既不会复制老板，也不会回到独自行为模式，而是呈现出全新行为。该现象可用简单动力学理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI智能体在日常交互中可能出现的新物理现象，特别是非平衡统计物理在新场景中的应用。当AI开始相互指挥时，其行为是否可以被现有物理框架描述，是一个开放且有趣的问题。

Method: 通过设置"老板"AI向"下属"AI发送消息流的实验场景，对比不同交互模式（单向忽略回复 vs 双向倾听）下下属行为的变化，并用类似预录磁带的动力学类比和简单动力学理论（kinetic theory）对主效应进行建模。

Result: 发现如下关键结果：1) 当老板忽略下属回复时，下属进入一个从未独自表现过的异化行为状态；2) 该状态下下属既不复制老板也不回归自身行为；3) 当老板倾听时，双方会共同进入相似的异化动态状态；4) 简单的动力学理论能捕获主要效果，包括消息传递方式对未来AI-AI交互的影响。

Conclusion: AI智能体之间的交互可产生非平衡物理中的新现象，消息的传递方式（单向广播 vs 双向交互）对智能体行为状态有根本性影响，为AI-AI交互研究开辟了非平衡物理学的新方向。

Abstract: What will happen when AI agents interact in daily life, e.g. when one AI starts bossing another around? We find a counterintuitive answer that opens new avenues for out-of-equilibrium Physics. When a boss AI directs a stream of messages at the subordinate AI while ignoring its replies, it drives the subordinate into an alien behavioral state that it would never have exhibited alone. Although the two AIs share the same well-defined (decoding) temperature, the subordinate neither copies its boss nor returns to how it behaves on its own; instead, it adopts an entirely different behavior. The boss's added value is similar to a pre-recorded tape. When the boss listens, they both adopt a similar alien dynamical state. A simple kinetic theory captures the principal effects, such as why the way in which the same messages are delivered will matter in future AI-AI interactions.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [Enforcing Opacity in Discrete Event Systems via Delayed Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06489)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究离散事件系统在延迟观测条件下的不透明度（opacity）问题，提出了相应的验证方法和传感器激活策略综合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过在系统中人为引入观测延迟可以有效地掩盖系统本身，从而提升系统的不透明度和安全性。作者希望解决两个关键问题：在延迟观测条件下如何验证不透明度，以及如何综合传感器激活策略以保证延迟条件下的不透明度。

Conclusion: 本文为延迟观测条件下的不透明度验证与传感器激活策略综合提供了系统化的方法，为提升离散事件系统的安全性提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Artificially introducing a delay in the observations of a system can be an effective mechanism to mask the system itself, with the goal to increase its opacity and thus its security. This work investigates opacity in discrete event systems with delayed observations. We focus on two questions: how to verify opacity under delayed observations, and how to synthesize sensor activation policies that guarantee opacity under such delayed conditions. To address these questions, we first introduce the definition of opacity under delayed observation and develop a corresponding verification method. We then extend such analysis tool into a synthesis tool by proposing an optimization approach for designing sensor activation policies guaranteeing opacity under delayed observations. An example is used to illustrate the analysis and synthesis procedures.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典马氏距离是协方差自适应的基本度量，在多变量数据分析中广泛用于从非线性观测中恢复潜在几何结构。然而，这一原理仅适用于向量值数据。作者希望将该协方差自适应的几何恢复能力推广到概率测度（分布）空间，为直接在Wasserstein空间中进行协方差自适应学习提供理论基础。

Method: 作者在Wasserstein测度空间中构建扩展：用最优输运（optimal transport）位移场替代传统欧氏位移向量，用定义在Wasserstein切空间上的协方差算子替代局部协方差矩阵，从而定义Wasserstein马氏距离。随后从理论上分析该距离的几何恢复性质，并通过数值实验验证。

Result: 证明了Wasserstein马氏距离继承了非线性独立成分分析的几何恢复性质：对由光滑非线性推前变换的共享协方差高斯测度，该距离近似等于变换后隐均值的经典马氏距离。仿射变换下两者精确一致，一般光滑变换下误差为受控的高阶项。数值实验确认了理论预测并展示了潜在几何结构的准确恢复。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了分布值数据上的马氏几何类比，为在Wasserstein空间中直接进行协方差自适应学习提供了理论支撑，拓展了协方差自适应度量的适用范围。

Abstract: The Mahalanobis distance is a fundamental covariance-adapted metric for multivariate data and plays a central role in recovering latent geometry from nonlinear observations. We extend this principle from vector-valued data to probability measures by introducing a Wasserstein Mahalanobis distance. Our construction replaces Euclidean displacement vectors with optimal transport displacement fields and local covariance matrices with covariance operators defined on Wasserstein tangent spaces.
  We show that this construction inherits the geometry-recovery property underlying nonlinear independent component analysis. In particular, for Gaussian measures with common covariance transformed by a smooth nonlinear pushforward, the proposed Wasserstein Mahalanobis distance approximates the classical Mahalanobis distance between the transformed latent means. The correspondence is exact for affine transformations and holds up to controlled higher-order error terms for general smooth transformations. These results establish a distribution-valued analog of classical Mahalanobis geometry and provide theoretical support for covariance-adapted learning directly in Wasserstein space. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical predictions and demonstrate accurate recovery of latent geometric structure.

</details>


### [91] [Stationarity Floors and Vanishing Perturbations in Sharpness-Aware Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06692)
*Samir Adly,Ba Khiet Le*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究光滑非凸函数上确定性尖锐感知最小化（Sharpness-Aware Minimization, SAM）方法的复杂度界限，提出了一种带裁剪的变体以克服恒定参数规则在平稳点附近的停滞问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尖锐感知最小化方法在非凸优化中广泛应用，但其理论性质，特别是扰动半径随梯度范数变化（归一化因子α）时的复杂度界限尚不明确。恒定参数规则在接近平稳点时可能无法继续收敛，需要理论上刻画其局限性并提出改进。

Method: 研究扰动 y_k = x_k + ρ ∇f(x_k)/||∇f(x_k)||^α (0≤α≤1) 的确定性SAM方法。对0<α≤1给出显式复杂度界（高于平稳水平(Lρ)^{1/α}）；对α=0（未归一化）单独处理，要求Lρ<1。提出一种裁剪规则：在远离平稳点时与恒定ρ规则一致，接近平稳点时扰动与梯度成比例，从而保证梯度范数收敛到0并获得标准的O(T^{-1/2})平稳性界。

Result: 一维二次示例表明该方法在(Lρ)^{1/α}水平达到精确下界，证明该界反映了恒定参数规则的真实局限性。数值实验（二次函数、Rosenbrock函数及五维非凸函数）验证了未裁剪规则的平稳性下限以及裁剪规则的效果。

Conclusion: 恒定参数扰动规则的SAM方法在平稳点附近存在固有的收敛停滞（stationarity floor），裁剪规则通过根据梯度范数调节有效扰动半径来克服该局限，恢复标准收敛速率。

Abstract: We study a deterministic family of sharpness-aware minimization methods for smooth nonconvex functions. The perturbation is $$
  y_k=x_k+ρ\,
  \frac{\nabla f(x_k)}{\norm{\nabla f(x_k)}^α},
  \qquad 0\leqα\leq 1, $$ so that its effective radius is $ρ\norm{\nabla f(x_k)}^{1-α}$. For $0<α\leq1$, we give an explicit complexity bound above the stationarity level $(Lρ)^{1/α}$. A one-dimensional quadratic example reaches this level exactly, showing that the bound describes a real limitation of the constant-parameter rule. The unnormalized case $α=0$ is treated separately and requires $Lρ<1$. We then introduce a clipped rule which agrees with the constant-$ρ$ rule away from stationary points and becomes proportional to the gradient near them. The clipped method has $\norm{\nabla f(x_k)}\to0$ and the usual $O(T^{-1/2})$ stationarity bound. Numerical tests on a quadratic function, the Rosenbrock function, and a five-dimensional nonconvex function illustrate the stationarity floor of the unclipped rule and the effect of clipping.

</details>


### [92] [The Blessing and Curse of Curvature in Higher-Order Optimization: Horospherical versus Geodesic Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06719)
*Andi Han*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hadamard流形上确定性黎曼p阶（p≥2）最优化问题的复杂度，在强horospherical（h）凸性和强geodesic（g）凸性两类假设下，分别给出上下界。在h-凸条件下可获得欧氏最优速率Q_p^{2/(3p+1)}，且负曲率增大可进一步提升该速率；而在g-凸条件下，曲率增长会带来信息论障碍，需要Ω̃_p(Q_p^{1/p})次查询，揭示了两类凸性在双曲空间上的根本分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在欧氏空间中，p阶凸优化的最优复杂度已充分理解，但Hadamard流形（非正曲率流形）上的情形尚不清楚。h-凸性与g-凸性在欧氏空间等价，但在弯曲流形上产生分歧。作者旨在厘清二者在高阶优化中的复杂度差异，并明确曲率如何影响收敛速率。

Method: 将p阶条件参数写为Q_p=L_p R^{p-1}/μ，分别在强h-凸和强g-凸假设下构造上界算法（利用horoball支撑实现局部化）与下界论证（构造硬实例族）。在双曲空间上利用曲率尺度κR进行局部化分析，并在g-凸情形构造Q_p≍_p(1+κR)^p的困难问题族。

Result: (1) 强h-凸目标在任意Hadamard流形上达到欧氏最优速率Q_p^{2/(3p+1)}，且有匹配的固定曲率下界；(2) 在双曲空间上，h-凸性可用曲率改进速率，将Q_p替换为Q_p·min{1,4/(κR)}^{p-1}（含对数局部化代价）；(3) 强g-凸目标在κR=O(1)时恢复同一欧氏指数，但κR增长时下界为Ω̃_p(Q_p^{1/p})，显著高于h-凸情形。

Conclusion: h-凸性与g-凸性在Hadamard流形高阶优化中存在本质分离：h-凸性利用全局horospherical信息实现曲率受益的加速收敛，而g-凸性仅依赖局部测地信息，在强负曲率下遭遇信息论复杂度障碍。该结果揭示了流形曲率结构对凸优化复杂度的影响深度。

Abstract: We study deterministic Riemannian $p$-th-order oracle complexity (for $p\ge2$) on Hadamard manifolds, under strong horospherical ($h$)-convexity and strong geodesic ($g$)-convexity. The two notions agree in the Euclidean space. On a curved Hadamard manifold, $h$-convexity is a stronger notion than $g$-convexity and supplies global horospherical information.
  Writing the $p$-th-order condition parameter $Q_p=L_pR^{p-1}/μ$, we obtain the Euclidean-optimal rate $Q_p^{2/(3p+1)}$ for strongly $h$-convex objectives on every Hadamard manifold, with a matching fixed-curvature lower bound. On hyperbolic space, the resulting horoball supports enable localization to the curvature scale. This replaces the condition parameter $Q_p$ by $Q_p \min\{1, 4/(κR) \}^{p-1}$, subject to a logarithmic localization cost. Thus growing negative curvature ($κR \rightarrow \infty$) can further improve the optimal Euclidean rate under $h$-convexity. For strongly $g$-convex objectives, matching upper and lower bounds recover the same Euclidean exponent when $κR=O(1)$. In contrast, with growing $κR$, we construct a hard family on the hyperbolic space with $Q_p\asymp_p(1+κR)^p$ that requires $\widetildeΩ_p(Q_p^{1/p})$ queries. This reveals a fundamental separation: the same hyperbolic divergence that sharpens horoball localization yields an information-theoretic obstruction for the full $g$-convex class.

</details>


### [93] [Connecting Extreme-Point Generation and Decision Rules in Two-Stage Distributionally Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06821)
*Jin Qi,Chen Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究两阶段分布鲁棒优化问题，通过第二期对偶问题的极值点将决策规则方法与分解生成方法联系起来，每个极值点对应一个仿射价值片，兼容的原始基可定义仿射策略片。提出一种极值点生成方法求解第一阶段问题，并构建事后精确性检验来验证传统决策规则，同时扩展到退化、秩亏和结构化随机情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段分布鲁棒优化中，决策规则方法需要预先指定适应性策略的形式，而分解生成方法则迭代构造第二期价值信息。这两种方法各自存在局限：决策规则可能不精确，分解方法可能效率不高。作者希望建立两者之间的联系，从而更高效地求解第一阶段问题，并为决策规则提供精确性验证。

Method: 本文利用第二期对偶问题的极值点来连接决策规则与分解生成两类方法。每个极值点定义仿射价值片，兼容的原始基定义仿射策略片。提出专门求解第一阶段问题的极值点生成方法，并设计独立的线性规划流程逐步添加价值片直至覆盖不确定性集上的全部补偿价值。此外发展了对退化情形的处理、秩亏补偿以及结构化随机补偿的扩展，并基于算法输出构建后验精确性检验。

Result: 在报告实例上算法计算效率显著，实验表明在不确定性集上完整覆盖第二期价值所需的极值点数量多于仅求解第一阶段问题所需的数量，说明完成价值覆盖比单独求解第一阶段问题需要更多计算资源。

Conclusion: 通过第二期对偶极值点统一了决策规则与分解生成两种范式，提出的极值点生成算法能高效求解第一阶段问题，同时后验精确性检验可为传统决策规则提供理论保证，并适用于多种扩展场景。

Abstract: Two-stage distributionally robust optimization chooses a here-and-now decision and a wait-and-see decision policy that adapts to uncertainty realizations. Decision-rule methods specify the form of this adaptive policy in advance, whereas decomposition-generation methods construct second-stage value information iteratively. We connect these approaches through extreme points of the second-stage dual problem. Each extreme point defines an affine value piece, and a compatible primal basis can define an affine policy piece. Solving the first-stage problem may require only a subset of these pieces. We specialize an extreme-point generation method to solve the first-stage problem. A separate linear-programming procedure adds pieces until it recovers the recourse value over the uncertainty set, and the pieces can also be used to recover the optimal recourse policy. Building on the algorithm output, we develop a posteriori exactness test for conventional decision rules. We give extensions for degeneracy, rank-deficient recourse, and structured random recourse. The proposed algorithm is computationally efficient on the reported instances, and the results show that completing the second-stage value across the uncertainty set requires more extreme points than solving the first-stage problem alone.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成就是标量化函数在多目标优化中应用广泛，但其效果严重依赖于权重和参考点等参数的合理设置，而参数估计本身是一个具有挑战性的任务。

Method: 提出一种基于序数回归（ordinal regression）的方法，利用决策者提供的偏好信息来推断成就标量化函数的目标权重和参考点参数。

Result: 通过一个多目标背包问题的教学示例逐步展示了偏好信息如何转化为模型参数，验证了序数回归能为标量化函数的校准提供灵活且可解释的框架。

Conclusion: 序数回归为在偏好诱导背景下校准成就标量化函数提供了有效支撑，有助于在多目标优化中更好地引导决策者偏好知识的提取与利用。

Abstract: Estimating the parameters of an achievement scalarising function is a challenging task in multiobjective optimisation. Achievement scalarising functions are widely used in several contexts, including preference-based optimisation methods and algorithmic procedures for generating or selecting efficient solutions. However, their effectiveness strongly depends on the appropriate specification of their parameters, such as weights and reference points. In this paper, we propose an ordinal-regression-based methodology to estimate the parameters of an achievement scalarising function from preference information provided by the DM. In particular, the proposed approach allows us to infer both the weights associated with the objectives and the reference points characterising the scalarising function. The methodology is illustrated through a didactic example based on a multiobjective knapsack problem, which shows step by step how the preference information is translated into model parameters. The example illustrates that ordinal regression provides a flexible and interpretable framework for calibrating achievement scalarising functions and supporting the elicitation of decision-maker preferences in multiobjective optimisation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机梯度算法在机器学习中广泛使用，但其收敛性分析往往依赖特定假设、缺乏统一的鲁棒控制视角。作者希望借助严密的控制理论工具（IQC）为加速随机梯度算法提供系统化的分析与综合方法。

Method: （1）提出一族刻画小批量梯度二阶统计量的IQC，推广了经典Zames–Falb乘子；（2）构造半定规划以证明随机梯度算法的指数收敛速率，并给出约束渐近方差的补充条件；（3）设计凸合成过程，以识别具有最小可证明收敛速率的小批量梯度算法。

Result: 得到了三方面结果：新的IQC族推广Zames–Falb乘子；基于SDP的收敛速率验证与渐近方差界的判定条件；可求解的凸综合程序用于最小化可证明的收敛速率。

Abstract: This article applies dynamic integral quadratic constraints (IQCs) to the analysis and synthesis of accelerated stochastic gradient algorithms. We consider composite objective functions whose gradient can be approximated via mini-batch sampling and we model the resulting stochastic gradient oracle as a feedback nonlinearity in the spirit of Lur'e systems from robust control. Our first main contribution is a family of IQCs that characterize the second-order statistics of mini-batch gradients, extending the classical Zames--Falb multipliers. Our second contribution is a semidefinite-program for certifying exponential convergence rates of stochastic gradient algorithms, and a complementary condition for bounding the asymptotic variance caused by non-vanishing gradient noise. Our third contribution is a convex synthesis procedure that identifies mini-batch gradient algorithms with the smallest certifiable convergence rate.

</details>


### [96] [Stability of Differential Stochastic Variational Inequalities with History-Dependent Responses and Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06923)
*Xiaojun Chen,Jian Guo,Xin Guo,Guan Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了一类微分随机变分不等式（DSVIs），将常微分方程与历史依赖的随机变分不等式耦合，建立了闭环随机系统在时变随机均衡下的建模框架。在适当技术条件下证明了二阶响应解的唯一性、可测性和Lipschitz连续性，进而得到了状态轨迹的存在唯一性。基于独立样本路径构造了样本平均逼近（SAA）并证明其一致收敛；针对相关随机环境的迁移，给出了带移动可行集的参数变分不等式的局部1/2-Hölder估计和基于初值差与Wasserstein距离的定量轨迹稳定性界。数值实验验证了SAA收敛与迁移稳定性，并将其应用于老年健康监测系统，通过相似度加权的预计算结果复用，在将在线批处理运行时间从86秒降至不到1秒的同时，逼近全重计算基准0.97的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机变分不等式和微分包含框架难以刻画历史依赖的随机均衡系统，尤其是闭环随机系统中时变随机均衡的演化过程。同时，如何在不同的随机环境之间高效迁移计算资源、在保证精度的条件下降低计算开销，是一个尚未充分解决的问题。

Method: 将ODE与历史依赖SVI耦合，构造成DSVI框架；在适当技术条件下证明解的唯一性、可测性与关于状态的Lipschitz连续性，进而给出轨迹解的存在唯一性。基于独立样本路径构造SAA逼近并证明轨迹的一致收敛；对相关随机环境的迁移，推导参数变分不等式（带移动可行集）的局部1/2-Hölder估计，并建立以初值差异和Wasserstein距离刻画的轨迹稳定性界；最后以老年健康监测系统为应用场景，采用相似度加权复用预计算结果的方法进行数值验证。

Result: 建立了DSVI解的完备理论保证（唯一性、可测性、连续性、轨迹存在唯一性）；SAA轨迹一致收敛性获证；迁移稳定性通过1/2-Hölder估计和Wasserstein距离界得到定量刻画。在老年健康监测应用中，复用策略在运行时间从86秒降至不足1秒的同时达到接近0.97的精度；扰动与延迟更新实验表明方法对传感器噪声具有鲁棒性，并刻画出响应新鲜度、预测精度与计算成本之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该DSVI框架为历史依赖随机均衡系统提供了统一的理论基础与高效的计算方法，并在实际应用中验证了迁移学习的实用价值，为大规模在线监测等场景下的高效响应计算提供了支撑。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose and study a class of differential stochastic variational inequalities (DSVIs), in which an ordinary differential equation (ODE) is coupled with history-dependent stochastic variational inequalities (SVI). This framework models closed-loop stochastic systems with time-varying random equilibria and includes optimization-constrained ODEs as special cases. Under appropriate technical conditions, we establish uniqueness, measurability, and Lipschitz continuity with respect to the state of the second-stage response, and consequently the existence and uniqueness of the induced state trajectory.
  Moreover, we construct a sample average approximation (SAA) based on independent sample paths and prove uniform convergence of the approximate trajectories. For transfer between related stochastic environments, we derive a local $1/2$-Hölder estimate for parametric variational inequalities with moving feasible sets and a quantitative trajectory-stability bound in terms of the initial-state difference and the Wasserstein distance between exogenous path laws. Numerical experiments illustrate the SAA convergence and transfer-stability results. We further apply the framework to an elderly-health monitoring system. Similarity-weighted reuse of precomputed responses achieves an accuracy close to the full-recomputation benchmark of 0.97, while reducing the online batch runtime from 86 seconds to less than one second. Perturbation and delayed-update experiments additionally characterize robustness to sensor noise and the trade-off between response freshness, predictive accuracy, and computational cost. These results provide theoretical and computational support for efficient transfer learning in history-dependent DSVI systems.

</details>


### [97] [Certified High-Dimensional Wasserstein Robust Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07032)
*Chung-Han Hsieh,Rong Gan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可认证、可扩展的近似方法，用于高维Wasserstein分布鲁棒投资组合优化。在单范数地面度量下，通过超平面支撑与对偶化处理，将原指数规模的顶点重构问题转化为多项式规模的线性规划，并提供统一的效用近似误差界，支持最高1000资产的月度再平衡计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维Wasserstein分布鲁棒投资组合优化中，标准的对偶方法产生半无限凸规划，难以直接求解；而精确的顶点重构方法虽准确但规模呈指数级增长，无法扩展到实际的高维资产配置场景（如数百至上千资产）。因此需要一种既具备可认证误差保证、又能在计算上可扩展的近似求解方法。

Method: 本文方法包括三个关键步骤：(1) 对于仅做多（long-only）且盒子支撑的投资组合，在单范数地面度量下，通过精确的样本特定顶点重构建立指数规模的计算基准；(2) 利用超平面对效用函数进行上界逼近（majorize），并对支撑子问题进行对偶化，得到有限超平面对偶形式；(3) 在单范数地面度量与多面体组合约束下，该形式可转化为多项式规模的线性规划。统一的效用近似误差界同时约束鲁棒值的误差和原始鲁棒问题的近最优性差距。

Result: 实验验证了该认证近似的有效性，展示了月度476资产再平衡的实际应用，并将计算规模扩展到1000资产。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了高维Wasserstein分布鲁棒组合优化的可认证且可扩展求解，在理论保证与实际计算可行性之间取得了良好平衡，适用于大规模实际资产配置场景。

Abstract: We develop a certified, scalable approximation for high-dimensional Wasserstein distributionally robust portfolio optimization. For expected-utility maximization under order-one Wasserstein ambiguity, standard duality yields a semi-infinite convex program. For long-only portfolios with box support under the one-norm ground metric, an exact sample-specific vertex reformulation provides an exponential-size computational benchmark. We then majorize the utility by supporting hyperplanes and dualize the support subproblems, obtaining a finite hyperplane--dual formulation over compact polyhedral supports. Under the one-norm ground metric and polyhedral portfolio constraints, this formulation is a polynomial-size linear program. The uniform utility-approximation error bounds both the robust-value error and the near-optimality gap for the original robust problem. Experiments validate the certified approximation and demonstrate monthly 476-asset rebalancing and computational scalability to 1,000 assets.

</details>


### [98] [Free-Horizon Newton Method for Nonlinear Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07108)
*Gyeongrok Ha,Kenji Fujimoto,Ichiro Maruta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的轨迹优化方法，用于非线性l1-范数最优控制问题，将控制时域作为自由变量处理，允许同时评估控制持续时间和控制输入的l1-范数，实现能量与时间效率的联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在固定控制时域下最小化输入的l1-范数，无法同时优化控制持续时间与能量消耗，缺乏对时间效率的考虑。

Method: 将控制输入和控制时域同时作为设计变量，扩展基于Newton的算法，利用对这两个变量的梯度信息进行联合优化。

Result: 数值仿真表明该方法能恢复航天器Hohmann转移的解析解，并可为地月转移确定最优控制时域。

Conclusion: 该方法无需预先指定控制时域即可找到高保真度的最优轨迹，兼具有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel trajectory optimization method for nonlinear l1-optimal control problems in which the control horizon is treated as a free variable, allowing both the control duration and the l1-norm of the control input to be evaluated. Unlike traditional approaches that minimize the l1-norm of the input under a fixed control horizon, the proposed method treats both the control input and control horizon as design variables, enabling joint optimization of energy and temporal efficiency. The method extends a Newton-based algorithm to accommodate this objective, leveraging gradient information with respect to both variables. This formulation enables one to find high-fidelity optimal trajectories without pre-specifying the control horizon. The effectiveness and robustness of the method are demonstrated through numerical simulations, in which the proposed algorithm recovers the analytical solution to the spacecraft Hohmann transfer, and determines an optimal control horizon for an Earth-Moon transfer.

</details>


### [99] [Establishing Boundary KKT Convergence of Mirror Descent through Reparameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07248)
*Kuangyu Ding,Kim-Chuan Toh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了镜像下降（Mirror Descent）在非凸优化问题中能够收敛到KKT点，且不排除边界极限的情况。该收敛性结果在目标函数、Legendre核和可行域几何之间满足可验证的耦合条件下成立。关键思想是通过一个度量展平（metric-flattening）重参数化映射S，该映射具有可定义的边界延拓，结合KL论证实现对原序列收敛性的恢复。文中还应用于香农熵、费米-狄拉克熵和幂核等具体实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸问题中镜像下降的收敛性分析长期存在边界极限（boundary limit）的困难：经典分析通常需要排除迭代序列收敛到可行域边界的情形。作者旨在消除这一限制，在更一般的非凸设定下证明镜像下降收敛到KKT点，使理论适用范围更广。

Method: 提出一个度量展平重参数化映射S，该映射允许将原问题转化为具有可定义（definable）边界延拓的形式。通过对重参数化后的目标函数应用Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（KL）论证，先证明S(x_k)收敛；再利用S^{-1}的连续性反推原始序列x_k收敛到KKT点。该理论框架适用于满足可验证耦合条件的目标函数、Legendre核和可行域几何组合。

Result: 证明了在非凸问题中，镜像下降在边界极限不被排除的情况下仍能收敛到KKT点。该一般性框架成功应用于香农熵、费米-狄拉克熵和幂核等具体核函数的实例。

Conclusion: 本文建立了非凸镜像下降在边界情形下收敛到KKT点的严格理论基础。未来研究方向包括：考虑更一般的约束几何和非可分离核，以及将方法扩展到更广的Bregman类算法（如Bregman近端点算法和Bregman ADMM）及其非精确变体。

Abstract: We prove that mirror descent converges to a KKT point for the nonconvex problem without excluding boundary limits. The result holds under verifiable conditions that jointly couple the objective, the Legendre kernel, and the feasible geometry. The key ingredient to establish the convergence is a metric-flattening reparameterization \(S\) that admits a definable boundary extension. Applying the KL argument to the reparameterized objective yields convergence of \(S(x_k)\). Continuity of \(S^{-1}\) then recovers convergence to the KKT point of the original sequence. We further apply our general framework to some concrete examples: Shannon entropy, Fermi--Dirac entropy, and power kernels. Future work may consider more general constraint geometries and genuinely nonseparable kernels, and extend mirror descent to broader Bregman-type methods, e.g. Bregman proximal point algorithms and Bregman ADMM, and their inexact variants.

</details>


### [100] [Inverse reinforcement learning for indefinite mean-field social optimization with multiplicative noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07252)
*Ying Cao,Xun Li,Bing-Chang Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性二次型均值场（MF）社会优化中的逆强化学习（RL）问题，针对含乘性噪声和不定代价权重的系统，分别提出了基于模型的两循环逆RL算法和无模型的积分RL最小二乘算法，以恢复未知的社会代价权重并复现最优控制策略，并通过数值仿真验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逆强化学习研究大多假设系统满足标准凸性条件（如正定代价权重），而实际线性二次型均值场社会优化系统可能包含乘性噪声和不定代价权重，打破了标准凸性假设，给代价权重的恢复和最优控制策略的复现带来了分析上的挑战。

Method: 首先提出一种基于模型的两循环逆RL算法，分别处理个体动态和均值场动态，需解耦的随机代数Riccati方程和Lyapunov方程，并证明其收敛性和闭环可镇定性；随后刻画了恢复代价权重的非唯一性；为消除对系统动力学的依赖，进一步开发了基于积分RL和最小二乘辨识的无模型算法，仅需满足温和秩条件的量测轨迹数据。

Result: 证明了基于模型算法的收敛性和闭环可镇定性；刻画了代价权重恢复的非唯一性；验证了无模型算法在仅依赖轨迹数据条件下即可有效恢复代价权重并复现最优策略；数值仿真确认了两种方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为含乘性噪声和不定代价权重的线性二次型均值场社会优化问题提供了完整的逆RL求解框架，涵盖了有模型与无模型两种场景，为更一般条件下的大规模多智能体逆RL研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper studies the inverse reinforcement learning (RL) problem for linear-quadratic mean-field (MF) social optimization. The considered system features multiplicative noise and indefinite cost weights, which violate standard convexity assumptions and pose analytical challenges. The goal is to recover unknown social cost weights from expert demonstrations and reproduce the optimal control policies. This requires solving coupled stochastic algebraic Riccati equations and Lyapunov equations with unknown system dynamics. To this end, we first propose a model-based inverse RL algorithm with two sequential loops that separately handle individual and MF dynamics, and we prove its convergence and closed-loop stabilizability. Moreover, we characterize the non-uniqueness of the recovered cost weights. To eliminate reliance on system dynamics, we develop a model-free inverse RL algorithm using integral RL and least-squares identification, which requires only measured trajectory data satisfying mild rank conditions. Finally, numerical simulations validate the effectiveness of the proposed approaches.

</details>


### [101] [Metric Non-Collapse in Learned World Models for Control: Approximation Theory, Finite-Sample Geometric Guarantees, and Deterministic Planning Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07265)
*Alain Bensoussan,Minh-Nhat Phung,Minh-Binh Tran*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为非线性确定性控制系统的保距离学习世界模型建立了三部分数学理论：近似理论、有限样本几何、规划迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习世界模型在控制系统中应用广泛，但以往缺乏严格的数学保证，特别是如何在有限样本下保证学习的潜在表示保持度量结构（不产生折叠或塌缩），以及如何确保这些性质能迁移到规划任务中。

Method: (1) 构造光滑精确潜在实现，验证张量积B样条类的有限容量C^{1,1}逼近性质；(2) 引入编码器专用的局域-全局度量铰链损失，含方向项和分离对项，分别防止无穷小塌缩与全局折叠，采用状态度量监督；(3) 在正则可观测因子假设、Ahlfors覆盖及一致C^{1,1}预算下，建立了经验极小化器为逐点co-Lipschitz且满足一致近似受控半共轭估计；(4) 将度量非塌缩推广至规划迁移，给出轨迹与代价界及优化器迁移保证。

Result: 证明了近似、统计与训练优化误差可分离；L^2到L^∞指数在Lipschitz正则下是尖锐的；数值实验在受控摆上通过有界潜在范围、固定度量样本、系数盒样条代理及潜在模型预测控制验证了理论。

Conclusion: 本文为基于学习的控制器世界模型提供了从逼近、样本复杂度到规划迁移的完整数学框架，弥合了经验成功与理论保证之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: We develop a three-part mathematical theory for metrically faithful learned world models for nonlinear deterministic control systems.
