First, on the approximation-theoretic side, we construct smooth exact latent realizations and verify the required finite-capacity $C^{1,1}$ approximation property for norm-constrained tensor-product B-spline classes with capacity-independent regularity budgets. Second, on the finite-sample geometric side, we introduce an encoder-only local--global metric hinge whose directional and separated-pair terms prevent infinitesimal collapse and global folding. Although the prediction loss is observation-based, evaluation of this penalty uses state-metric supervision through observable-state distances and tangent directions. Under a regular observable-factor assumption, lower-Ahlfors coverage, and uniform $C^{1,1}$ budgets, provided the explicit finite-sample deviation lies below the metric-margin threshold, every approximate empirical minimizer above a computable one-sided regularization threshold is pointwise co-Lipschitz and satisfies a uniform approximate controlled-semiconjugacy estimate, with approximation, statistical, and training-optimization errors kept separate. The $L^2$-to-$L^\infty$ exponent used in this step is sharp under Lipschitz regularity. Third, for deterministic planning transfer, metric non-collapse induces compatible Lipschitz latent costs, while semiconjugacy gives uniform trajectory and finite-horizon cost bounds and an optimizer-transfer guarantee; learned latent cost heads enter through explicit compatibility errors. Numerical experiments use bounded latent ranges, archived fixed metric samples, a finite-capacity coefficient-box spline proxy motivated by the theorem, and latent model-predictive control on a controlled pendulum.

</details>


### [102] [On the Optimal Laplacian Jordan Structure for Synchronizability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07286)
*Luca Dieci,Cinzia Elia,Alessandro Pugliese*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究如何为具有给定图结构的微分方程网络选择Laplacian矩阵，以同时满足三个同步性优化目标：保证同步解的渐近稳定性、最小化归一化Laplacian特征值散布、以及最小化瞬态持续时间。在三对角Laplacian类中给出了满足这三项标准的充要条件，并将其推广至带状Laplacian，最后通过大量数值实验验证理论结果并与现有工作对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂网络同步性研究中，Laplacian矩阵的选择直接影响网络的同步能力。现有工作通常仅关注单一优化目标（如特征值比或代数连通度），缺乏对多个同步性指标（稳定性、特征值散布、瞬态长度）的综合考虑与联合优化。

Method: 作者在三对角Laplacian矩阵的受限类别内，基于主稳定函数（master stability function）框架，推导了满足渐近稳定性、最小化归一化特征值散布以及最短瞬态三个条件的充要条件；随后将结果扩展至带状Laplacian矩阵，并通过数值实验加以验证。

Result: 给出了三对角Laplacian类中同时满足三项同步性标准的充要条件，延伸至带状Laplacian结构，并通过大量数值实验验证了理论结果的正确性，且与已有文献中的方法进行了对比。

Conclusion: 该工作系统性地解决了Laplacian选择中的多目标优化问题，为网络拓扑配置提供了理论依据，并展示了在三对角及更一般的带状Laplacian结构下可实现最优同步性能。

Abstract: In this work, for a network of differential equations with a prescribed graph structure, our goal is to show how to select the Laplacian of the network in order to obtain the most favorable outcome insofar as synchronizability. That is, we will want to: (i) guarantee asymptotic stability of a synchronous solution (as measured by a negative value of the master stability function), (ii) minimize the normalized spread of the Laplacian eigenvalues, and (iii) have a transient as short as possible. Within the class of tridiagonal Laplacians, we give both necessary and sufficient conditions for satisfying our three criteria above, and give extension to banded Laplacians as well. Finally, we give extensive numerical results to elucidate our theoretical results and to compare to existing works.

</details>


### [103] [Sampling and Optimization meet Enhanced Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07329)
*Yuan Gao,Siming He,Eitan Tadmor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对Gibbs概率测度的计算实现问题，引入两类具有快速收敛性的动力学，其快速收敛机制源于输运-扩散动力学中的增强耗散效应。基于这些增强动力学设计了数值采样算法，并给出了相应的粒子系统以提供其他有效的数值采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs概率测度（$e^{-\mathbb{U}(\mathbf{x})}/Z$）的计算实现在采样与优化中具有核心地位，现有方法在收敛速度上存在瓶颈，需要开发具有快速收敛特性的动力学方法。

Method: 提出两类输运-扩散动力学，利用增强耗散（enhanced dissipation）机制驱动对Gibbs测度的快速收敛；在此基础上设计相应的数值采样算法，并进一步构造对应的粒子系统。

Result: 证明了所提出的两类动力学对Gibbs测度具有快速收敛性，并基于这些动力学设计出数值采样算法及粒子系统。

Conclusion: 增强耗散机制为Gibbs测度的高效采样提供了新途径，所提出的动力学、数值算法和粒子系统为采样与优化领域提供了可行的新工具。

Abstract: It is well known that the computational realization of Gibbs probability measures, $e^{-\mathbb{U}(\mathbf{x})}/Z$, plays a central role in sampling and optimization. In this paper, we introduce two types of dynamics that exhibit rapid convergence towards these Gibbs measures. The mechanism driving this rapid convergence is the enhanced dissipation associated with these transport-diffusion dynamics. Motivated by these enhanced dynamics, we design numerical algorithms for sampling from the target Gibbs measure. Finally, we provide the corresponding particle systems that may yield other effective numerical samplers.

</details>


### [104] [Solving Monotone Linear-Quadratic Generalized Nash Equilibrium Problems via Quadratic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07336)
*Alberto Bemporad,Tatiana Tatarenko*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究N个玩家、凸二次成本和共享仿射约束的广义纳什均衡问题（仅假设伪梯度单调），证明求解变分广义纳什均衡等价于求解单个凸二次规划（QP），并基于此提出加速近端点方法和加速投影梯度方法，均达到O(1/k²)的近似v-GNE收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的求解单调线性二次v-GNE问题的方法收敛速度较慢或假设条件过强，本文希望利用问题的二次结构，将v-GNE求解转化为单个凸QP，从而开发加速算法以显著超越现有方法。

Method: 首先利用所有玩家的联合KKT条件将v-GNE问题等价转化为单个凸QP；然后对该QP进行正则化得到ε-近似v-GNE（次优性随正则化参数线性衰减）；进一步提出加速近端点方案和加速投影梯度方法，二者在第k次迭代达到O(1/k²)的近似v-GNE；当博弈的Jacobian可逆时，可将问题降维到更低维度的QP。

Result: 理论分析表明所提方法在第k次迭代达到O(1/k²)的近似v-GNE收敛率，且正则化参数与次优性呈线性关系。数值实验验证了所提方法在求解单调线性二次v-GNE问题上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过将v-GNE问题等价转化为单个凸QP，本文提出的加速算法在理论收敛率和实际数值表现上都大幅优于现有方法，为单调线性二次v-GNE问题提供了高效求解途径。

Abstract: We consider generalized Nash equilibrium problems among $N$ players with convex quadratic costs and shared affine constraints, assuming only that the game's pseudogradient is merely monotone. We show that computing a variational generalized Nash equilibrium (v-GNE) is equivalent to solving a single convex quadratic program (QP) derived from the players' joint Karush--Kuhn--Tucker conditions. Building on this, we show that the regularization of such a QP yields an $\varepsilon$-approximated v-GNE with suboptimality vanishing linearly in the regularization parameter. Next, we propose an accelerated proximal-point scheme and an accelerated projected-gradient method, both attaining an $\mathcal O(1/k^2)$-approximated v-GNE at the $k$-th iteration. We also demonstrate that an invertible Jacobian of the game allows for reduction to a lower-dimensional QP. Theoretical analysis and numerical experiments show the proposed methods substantially outperform the existing approaches to solve monotone linear-quadratic v-GNE problems.

</details>


### [105] [Uncovering expert objectives in production planning via inverse optimization: An industrial case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07398)
*Shivi Dixit,Rishabh Gupta,Adam Kelloway,John Wassick,Qi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的逆优化框架，用于从专家规划者的历史决策中推断隐式的目标函数。该方法将生产计划问题建模为混合整数线性规划，通过次优损失逆优化方法学习目标权重，并在Dow公司的真实工业案例中验证，发现避免库存短缺和保持周期一致性主导规划者决策，专家访谈确认了结论的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造业生产规划常依赖优化模型，但定义合适的目标函数极具挑战。实践中规划者需平衡竞争目标、管理不确定性并考虑难以量化的定性业务偏好，导致许多优化模型无法匹配专家行为，限制了信任与采用。因此需要一种方法从专家决策中自动学习目标函数。

Method: 将生产规划问题建模为混合整数线性规划（MILP），未知目标函数表示为假设成本项的加权和；采用基于次优损失（suboptimality-loss）的逆优化方法，从历史生产计划中学习目标权重；扩展到时间和产品相关的变体以提升预测精度并揭示偏好演化。

Result: 应用于Dow公司的真实工业案例，推断出的权重显示避免库存短缺和维持一致的周期长度主导规划者决策；时间和产品相关的扩展进一步提升了预测精度，并揭示了规划者偏好的演变；专家访谈确认了这些洞察的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 逆优化能够将隐性的人类专业知识转化为可解释的模型，为复杂工业系统构建更准确、更受信任的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Production planning in the manufacturing industry often relies on the use of optimization models, but defining an appropriate objective function can be a challenge. In practice, planners must balance competing goals, manage uncertainty, and account for qualitative business preferences that are difficult to quantify. As a result, many optimization models fail to match expert behavior, limiting trust and adoption. In this work, we propose a data-driven inverse optimization framework to infer the objective function implicitly captured in expert planners' decisions. We formulate the production planning problem as a mixed-integer linear program, where the unknown objective function is represented as a weighted sum of hypothesized cost terms. A suboptimality-loss-based inverse optimization method is then applied to learn the objective weights from historical production plans. The proposed approach is applied to a real industrial case provided by Dow, where the inferred weights reveal that avoiding inventory shortages and maintaining consistent cycle lengths dominate the planners' decision-making. Time- and product-dependent extensions further improve predictive accuracy and uncover evolving priorities. Expert interviews confirm the practical validity of these insights. Overall, this study shows that inverse optimization can transform tacit human expertise into interpretable models, enabling more accurate and trusted decision-support tools for complex industrial systems.

</details>


### [106] [A Domain-Specific Harness for End-to-End Automation of Optimization Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07407)
*Heechang Kim,Ernest K. Ryu,Shuvomoy Das Gupta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoOPT，一个面向优化研究端到端自动化的领域专用框架，将最优一阶方法的发现组织为四个阶段：BnB-PEP数值设计、大语言模型符号发现与收敛证明、Lean 4形式化验证、以及人工解读与撰写。通过两个案例验证了框架能力：新方法"lemniscate加速"达到O(1/N^4)的梯度范数平方最优收敛率；以及ITEM-f方法的解析描述。两者的收敛定理均在Lean 4中完成机器检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化方法研究传统上依赖人工推导与数值实验，发现新方法（尤其是最优一阶方法）的过程耗时且易出错。近年来性能估计问题（PEP）和分支定界（BnB-PEP）等方法虽能数值地发现最优方法，但缺乏从数值结果到解析描述、证明、验证的自动化链路。作者希望构建一个端到端自动化框架，将数值发现、符号推理、形式化验证有机整合，从而加速并提高优化研究的可靠性与可复现性。

Method: AutoOPT框架分为四个阶段：(1) 数值设计阶段，利用BnB-PEP方法论数值求解性能估计问题以发现最优方法的结构；(2) 符号发现阶段，借助前沿大语言模型（LLMs）从数值结果中推导方法的解析描述并生成收敛证明；(3) 形式化验证阶段，在Lean 4证明助手中对收敛定理进行机器检查；(4) 人工解读与撰写阶段，由研究人员对结果进行解释并整理成文。框架在两个案例上演示：lemniscate加速（针对光滑凸函数梯度范数最小化的新加速梯度方法）和ITEM-f的解析描述。

Result: 案例一：提出了lemniscate加速方法，经过N步梯度迭代后，将光滑凸函数的平方梯度范数以最优的O(1/N^4)速率降低，其常数由经典椭圆积分常数——lemniscate常数ϖ控制。案例二：给出了此前仅数值已知的ITEM-f方法的解析描述，该方法在L-光滑、μ-强凸最小化问题上以每个因子(1−√(μ/L))^2加速线性速率收缩函数值差距。两个案例的收敛定理均已在Lean 4中形式化并机器验证。

Conclusion: AutoOPT展示了将数值优化方法发现与LLM符号推理及形式化验证相结合的可行性，开辟了优化研究自动化的新范式。该方法不仅发现了新的最优方法（lemniscate加速），还完成了此前仅数值已知方法（ITEM-f）的解析刻画，且所有理论结果均经过机器可验证的严格证明。这为优化领域的加速研究提供了一个可扩展的自动化管道。

Abstract: We present AutoOPT, a domain-specific harness for end-to-end automation of optimization research. AutoOPT organizes the discovery of optimal first-order methods into four stages: numerical design through the BnB-PEP methodology; symbolic discovery of the analytic description and a convergence proof through frontier large language models (LLMs); formal verification in the Lean 4 proof assistant; and human interpretation and write-up. We demonstrate the framework on two case studies, each of independent interest. The first, lemniscate acceleration, is a new accelerated gradient method for minimizing the gradient norm of a smooth convex function: after $N$ gradient steps it reduces the squared gradient norm at the optimal $O(1/N^{4})$ rate, with a constant governed by the lemniscate constant $\varpi$, a classical elliptic-integral constant. The second is the analytic description of ITEM-f, a method previously known only numerically: for $L$-smooth, $μ$-strongly convex minimization it contracts the function-value gap at an accelerated linear rate with a per-step factor $(1-\sqrt{μ/L})^{2}$. The convergence theorems of both case studies are formalized and machine-checked in Lean 4.

</details>


### [107] [Wasserstein Policy Gradient for Entropy-Regularized Linear-Quadratic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07433)
*Zhaoyu Zhu,Rui Gao,Shuang Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究熵正则化的折扣线性二次（LQ）控制问题中的Wasserstein策略梯度（WPG）算法。通过Bellman验证论证证明无约束问题具有线性-高斯最优策略，且WPG梯度恰好落在该策略类上，从而将WPG精确约化为有限维ODE。文章证明该ODE全局适定且从任意可容许初始化均指数收敛，收敛指数在熵温度趋于零时存在正极限，且不包含exp(-c/τ)形式的微扰因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Wasserstein策略梯度（WPG）在具有连续状态和动作空间的强化学习问题中的理论性质，尤其是其收敛性和与最优策略结构的关系。LQ控制作为经典且可解析处理的基准模型，为研究WPG的动态行为提供了理想的测试平台。

Method: 采用Bellman验证论证建立熵正则化LQ问题的线性-高斯最优策略结构；利用折扣占据权重下的状态条件Wasserstein梯度与线性-高斯策略类相切的几何性质，将无穷维WPG更新精确约化为关于反馈增益和动作协方差的有限维常微分方程（ODE），进而对该ODE进行全局适定性和指数收敛性分析。

Result: 证明了WPG在熵正则化LQ问题中可精确约化为有限维ODE；该ODE从任意可容许初始化出发全局适定并指数收敛到最优策略；收敛指数在温度参数τ→0时具有正极限，不包含exp(-c/τ)的小参数微扰因子，仅保留对控制问题条件数的依赖。

Conclusion: WPG算法在LQ控制框架下具有精确的有限维结构描述和全局指数收敛保证，且其收敛率在低熵极限（低温）下不会退化到指数级慢，说明WPG在熵正则化设置中具有良好的收敛性质，同时受问题条件数制约。

Abstract: Wasserstein policy gradient (WPG) updates state-conditional action laws by transport in the action space. We study entropy-regularized discounted linear-quadratic (LQ) control. A Bellman verification argument shows that the unrestricted problem has a linear-Gaussian optimal policy, and the discounted-occupancy-weighted statewise Wasserstein gradient is tangent to this policy class. WPG therefore reduces exactly to a finite-dimensional ODE for the feedback gain and action covariance. We prove that this ODE is globally well posed and converges exponentially from every admissible initialization. For each fixed LQ problem, the exponent has a positive limit as the entropy temperature tends to zero and contains no perturbative factor of the form $\exp(-c/τ)$, while retaining the usual dependence on the conditioning of the control problem.

</details>


### [108] [A proximal subgradient method for nonconvex stochastic optimization under the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05460)
*Felipe Atenas,Alejandro Jofré,Pedro Pérez-Aros,David Torregrosa-Belén*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种近端随机次梯度方法，用于最小化期望成本（其被积函数可能非光滑且非凸）与下半连续prox-bounded函数之和。该方法基于非光滑局部化下降引理，采用逐步细化的样本均值近似和Armijo型线搜索，在仅要求样本数量非降且无界（无需预定增长率）的宽松条件下，建立了函数值几乎必然收敛和累积点平稳性的保证，并借助KL性质升级为整条轨迹收敛，对指数型KL去奇函数和多形式增长的样本量导出了显式多项式收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理非光滑、非凸的随机优化问题时存在诸多限制：需要正则项的（弱）凸性假设，或者要求随机预言机方差有界。本文旨在放宽这些假设，构建一个更通用的框架，同时提升收敛性理论保证的质量。

Method: 提出近端随机次梯度方法，核心要素包括：（1）被积函数满足非光滑、局部化的下降引理变体，涵盖光滑Lipschitz梯度损失与凸函数之差；（2）用逐步细化的样本均值替代期望成本；（3）采用Armijo型线搜索选择近端次梯度步长，实现直到随机误差的充分下降性质；（4）利用KL性质提升收敛性结论并推导收敛速率。

Result: 在样本数量序列仅需非降且无界的宽松条件下，建立了函数值序列的几乎必然收敛以及轨迹所有累积点的平稳性。借助KL性质，将子序列收敛升级为整条轨迹收敛到单一平稳点。对于指数型KL去奇函数和多项式增长的样本量，导出了函数值和迭代点的显式多项式收敛速率（含对数因子）。这些收敛性保证即使在光滑设置下也是新的。

Conclusion: 该框架显著推广了现有方法，不要求正则项的（弱）凸性，也不要求随机预言机方差的均匀有界，能够处理比现有方法更为一般的问题表述。其收敛性分析在光滑情形中也是新结果，理论保证在样本量增长的放宽条件和KL框架下的收敛速率方面均有实质性进展。

Abstract: This work introduces a proximal stochastic subgradient method for minimizing the sum of an expected cost, whose integrand is potentially nonsmooth and nonconvex, and a lower semicontinuous, prox-bounded function. We target a broad class of integrands obeying a nonsmooth, localized variant of the descent lemma in the decision variable, a structural assumption that simultaneously covers smooth losses with Lipschitz gradient and differences of such losses with convex functions. At each iteration the expected cost is replaced by a sample average that is progressively refined, and the proximal-subgradient stepsize is selected by an Armijo-type line search enforcing a sufficient-decrease property up to stochastic errors induced by the sample-based approximation. This framework accommodates substantially more general problem formulations than existing methods, in particular, it requires neither (weak) convexity of the regularizer nor a uniform bound on the variance of the stochastic oracle, and our analysis yields convergence guarantees that are new even in the smooth setting. Specifically, we establish almost sure convergence of the sequence of function values and stationarity of every accumulation point of the trajectories under the relaxed requirement that the sample-size sequence be merely nondecreasing and unbounded, with no prescribed growth rate. Leveraging the Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL) property, we further upgrade this subsequential guarantee to convergence of the whole trajectory to a single stationary point. Finally, for exponential-type KL desingularizing functions and polynomially growing sample sizes, we derive explicit polynomial convergence rates, up to a logarithmic factor, for both the function values and the iterates.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Mixture of Geodesic Factor Analyzers on Riemannian Homogeneous Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06971)
*Hengchao Chen,Yuanyao Tan,Chao Huang,Hongtu Zhu,Qiang Sun*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在黎曼齐性空间上的测地线因子分析器混合模型（MGFA），在每个混合分量中使用测地线因子模型，比混合黎曼径向分布更具表达力，能够对具有各向异性子群体的流形值数据进行聚类。论文建立了MGFA最大似然估计的根号n一致性，提出了迭代估计算法，并在球面、形状空间和双曲空间上进行了实现和验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的混合黎曼径向分布模型虽然能够对流形值数据进行聚类，但其各向同性的假设限制了表达力，无法有效捕捉数据中各向异性的子群体结构。本文旨在填补这一理论和方法上的空白，提出更具表达力的模型。

Method: 在黎曼齐性空间的每个混合分量中引入测地线因子模型，建立MGFA的极大似然估计理论框架并证明根号n一致性；提出迭代估计算法，在球面、形状空间和双曲空间上实现。

Result: 数值实验表明，在模型设定正确的情况下MGFA显著优于竞争方法，且在模型误设下仍保持鲁棒性；在胼胝体和左侧海马体形状数据集上的案例研究验证了其对2D轮廓和3D形状分析的有效性。

Conclusion: MGFA为流形值数据的聚类和形状分析提供了更强大的工具，兼具理论保证和实际有效性，特别适用于具有各向异性子群体的复杂形状数据分析场景。

Abstract: This paper introduces Mixtures of Geodesic Factor Analyzers (MGFA) on Riemannian homogeneous spaces. MGFA uses a geodesic factor model within each mixture component, providing greater expressiveness than mixtures of Riemannian radial distributions and enabling clustering of manifold-valued data with anisotropic subpopulations. We establish root-$n$ consistency for the MGFA maximum likelihood estimator (MLE), thereby filling a theoretical gap for mixtures of Riemannian radial distributions as a special case. We also propose an iterative estimation algorithm and implement it on spheres, shape spaces, and hyperbolic spaces. Numerical experiments show that MGFA substantially outperforms competing methods in well-specified regimes while remaining robust under model misspecification. Finally, case studies on corpus callosum and left hippocampus shape datasets demonstrate MGFA's effectiveness for both 2D contour and 3D shape analysis.

</details>


### [110] [Optimized Certainty Equivalent Risk Minimization Using Samples: Algorithms, Convergence Rates, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07113)
*Sumedh Gupte,Prashanth L. A.,Sanjay P. Bhat*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统地研究了优化确定性等价（OCE）风险的优化问题，涵盖了金融投资组合优化和机器学习中的不确定性量化、分类与回归等应用。作者建立了OCE与基于效用的短期风险（UBSR）之间的有利联系，据此构建了OCE估计器及其梯度估计器，推导了非渐近均方误差（MSE）界，并将梯度估计器嵌入随机梯度（SG）算法中以优化OCE，最后通过三个实验验证了所提算法在投资组合优化和不确定性量化中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCE风险度量涵盖了熵风险、均值-方差风险和条件风险价值（CVaR）的平滑变体等众多流行特例，在金融和机器学习领域具有广泛的应用前景。然而，OCE的优化问题尚未被系统解决，特别是将其扩展到无界随机变量、构建高效的估计器和梯度估计器，以及量化优化算法的收敛性能方面仍存在空白。

Method: (1) 给出将OCE扩展至无界随机变量的条件；(2) 建立OCE与UBSR之间的联系特性，为后续估计与优化奠定基础；(3) 基于UBSR的经典样本平均近似（SAA）构造OCE估计器并推导其MSE界；(4) 利用OCE-UBSR联系推导OCE梯度的解析表达式，据此构建梯度估计器并给出非渐近MSE界；(5) 将梯度估计器引入随机梯度算法优化OCE，推导算法的非渐近收敛速率界；(6) 通过投资组合优化和不确定性量化三个实验验证所提方法的实际性能。

Result: 作者成功推导了OCE估计器和OCE梯度估计器的非渐近均方误差界，以及基于随机梯度算法的OCE优化非渐近收敛速率界。三个实验表明所提OCE优化算法在投资组合优化和不确定性量化问题上表现有效。

Conclusion: 本文通过建立OCE与UBSR之间的桥梁，为OCE风险优化提供了一套完整的理论框架：包括可处理的扩展条件、基于SAA的估计方法、梯度估计器以及带有收敛保证的随机优化算法，在理论上补齐了OCE优化的系统性分析，在实践上为金融和机器学习的风险敏感优化提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We consider the optimization of the Optimized Certainty Equivalent (OCE) risk, with applications including portfolio optimization in finance, and uncertainty quantification, classification, and regression in machine learning. Our contributions cover popular special cases of OCE, such as entropic risk, mean-variance risk, and smooth variants of Conditional Value-at-Risk. Our treatment sets out the conditions that facilitate the extension of OCE to unbounded r.v.s.. We provide a useful characterization of OCE that links OCE to utility-based shortfall risk (UBSR). Our characterization enables us to form an OCE estimator from the classic sample-average approximation (SAA) of UBSR. We derive mean-squared error (MSE) bounds for our proposed OCE estimator. For OCE optimization, we first derive an expression for the OCE gradient using the characterization linking OCE to UBSR. This expression serves as the basis for a gradient estimator for the OCE. We derive non-asymptotic bounds on the MSE for the proposed OCE gradient estimator. We incorporate the aforementioned gradient estimator into a stochastic gradient (SG) algorithm to optimize OCE and quantify its convergence rate using non-asymptotic bounds that we derive. Finally, we present three experiments that use our OCE optimization algorithm to solve portfolio optimization and uncertainty quantification problems.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [Robust Average-Reward Markov Decision Processes: Minimax-Optimal Learning via Plug-in Reductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06545)
*Yuepeng Yang,Yuxin Chen,Yuejie Chi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在平均报酬准则下，分布鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（RMDP）学习ε-最优鲁棒策略所需的样本复杂度。在生成模型提供的名义转移核样本和(s,a)-矩形全变差不确定性集下，作者识别出σH₀作为划分高/低容忍度区域的摄动尺度，并给出了匹配的上下界，证明最小极大样本复杂度在两种区域下分别由线性跨度项和鲁棒性特定项组成，且通过两种基于归约的插值程序（跨度已知和跨度未知）可实现这些速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布鲁棒MDP为模型不确定性下的序贯决策提供了原则性框架，但此前关于其样本复杂度的研究主要聚焦于折扣准则或有限范围设定，而平均报酬准则下的样本效率尚未被充分理解。本文旨在回答需要多少样本才能在平均报酬准则下学习到ε-最优鲁棒策略，从而填补该理论空白。

Method: 作者从理论上分析（s,a）-矩形全变差不确定性集下RMDP的极小极大样本复杂度。首先通过理论分析识别出σH₀作为关键摄动尺度，区分高容忍度（ε≳σH₀）和低容忍度（ε≲σH₀）两种区域。然后提出两种归约式插值算法：跨度知情程序（利用已知H₀和H_σ选择归约类型和折扣因子）和跨度未知程序（从数据自适应校准这两个选择）。通过匹配的上下界给出完整的最优样本复杂度刻画。

Result: 证明了在忽略对数因子下，总样本复杂度NSA ≍ SA/ε² × [min{H₀,H_σ}（高容忍度时）或 min{H₀,H_σ}+σH_σ²（低容忍度时）]。该复杂度由类似名义AMDP结果的线性跨度项和仅在低容忍度区域出现的鲁棒性特定项组成。上下界匹配，达到了极小极大最优。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了平均报酬准则下RMDP的样本复杂度，揭示了σH₀作为区域划分的关键阈值，并提供了实现最优速率的算法（包含跨度已知和跨度未知两种版本）。这为鲁棒序贯决策的样本效率提供了精确的理论理解。

Abstract: Distributionally robust Markov decision processes provide a principled framework for sequential decision making under model uncertainty. We study how many samples are necessary and sufficient to learn an $\varepsilon$-optimal robust policy under the average-reward criterion. A generative model provides samples from the nominal transition kernel, whereas policy performance is evaluated over $(s,a)$-rectangular total-variation uncertainty sets of radius at most $σ$.
  Let $H_0$ and $H_σ$ denote the nominal and robust optimal bias spans, respectively. We identify $σH_0$ as the perturbation scale separating high- and low-tolerance regimes. Our matching upper and lower bounds show that, up to logarithmic factors, the minimax total sample complexity is $$ NSA \asymp \frac{SA}{\varepsilon^2}\begin{cases} \min\{H_0,H_σ\}, & \varepsilon\gtrsimσH_0,\\ \min\{H_0,H_σ\}+σH_σ^2, & \varepsilon\lesssimσH_0. \end{cases} $$ Here $S$ and $A$ are the numbers of states and actions, and $N$ is the number of samples per state-action pair. The sample complexity consists of a linear-span term that resembles the nominal AMDP results and a robustness-specific term that appears only in the low-tolerance regime. We attain these rates using reduction-based plug-in procedures that select the reduction---nominal or robust---and its discount factor: a span-informed procedure that makes these choices using known span parameters, and a span-agnostic procedure that calibrates both choices from data.

</details>


### [112] [Latent Fact-Checking: Detecting Misinformation through Activation Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06417)
*Pedro Barcelos,Otávio Parraga,Marcelo M. Mussi,Lucas M. Fraga,Lucas S. Kupssinskü,Rodrigo C. Barros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于激活工程（activation engineering）的虚假信息检测框架，利用Transformer语言模型潜在空间中"真实性"的几何结构，通过对比真实与虚假陈述的激活差异提取"虚假方向"，在推理时将未验证声明的最后token激活投影到该方向并用MLP分类。该方法无需微调、外部知识检索或任务级监督，在11个模型和3个基准上的实验表明该方向可跨模型规模与架构恢复，在小模型上性能最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚假信息检测主要依赖表层语言特征或外部知识检索，前者捕捉力有限，后者依赖外部资源且扩展成本高。作者质疑现有范式，转而探索语言模型表示空间中真实性是否具有可操作的几何结构，旨在提供一个无需检索、无需微调、高效可扩展的检测方案。

Method: 将检测建模为激活工程问题：(1) 通过配对真实/虚假陈述的残差流激活对比，基于Contrastive Activation Addition（CAA）的均值差原理提取"虚假方向"；(2) 推理时将未验证声明的最后token激活投影到该方向；(3) 将投影后的表征输入MLP分类器。全程无需微调骨干模型、无需外部证据检索、无需任务级标注（仅需对比语句对估计方向）。

Result: 在Gemma、Llama、Qwen三个系列共11个模型（270M至12B参数）上，针对AVeriTeC、LIAR、FACTors三个基准进行评估。虚假方向可跨模型规模和架构恢复；在LIAR和FACTors上，最后token投影匹配或超越零样本和少样本提示基线，小模型收益最大；在AVeriTeC上表现有限，归因于其基于证据的标注方案。

Conclusion: 实验证据表明，预训练语言模型的潜在空间中真实性是一种结构化的、线性可分的概念。这为可解释性驱动的虚假信息检测提供了实用路径，可作为基于检索的流水线的补充方案。

Abstract: The proliferation of misinformation online has driven demand for scalable detection systems. While most existing approaches rely on surface-level linguistic features or external knowledge retrieval, we examine truthfulness as a geometric property of a language model's representation space. We introduce a misinformation detection framework grounded in activation engineering, which leverages the latent geometry of transformer models. Our approach elicits a misinformation direction in the residual stream by contrasting activations from paired truthful and false statements, following the difference-in-means principle of Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA). At inference time, the last-token activation of an unseen claim is projected onto this direction, and the projected representation is fed to an Multilayer Perceptron (MLP) for classification. The procedure requires no fine-tuning of the backbone model, no external evidence retrieval, and no task-specific supervision beyond the contrastive pairs used to estimate the direction. We evaluate the method across 11 models from the Gemma, Llama, and Qwen families, ranging from 270M to 12B parameters, on three fact-checking benchmarks: AVeriTeC, LIAR, and FACTors. The falsehood direction is recoverable across model scales and architectural families, and last-token projection matches or surpasses zero-shot and few-shot prompting baselines on LIAR and FACTors, with the largest gains observed for smaller models. Performance on AVeriTeC is more limited, which we attribute to its evidence-grounded labeling scheme. These findings provide evidence that truthfulness is a structured, linearly separable concept in the latent space of pretrained language models, and point toward interpretability-driven misinformation detection as a practical complement to retrieval-based pipelines. The code is available on https://github.com/Malta-Lab/LaFaCt.

</details>


### [113] [Omni-modal decomposition autoencoders learn full-stack wearable disentangled representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07385)
*Ioannis Ziogas,Ensieh Khazaei,Bilal Taha,Aamna Al Shehhi,Ahsan H. Khandoker,Leontios J. Hadjileontiadis,Dimitrios Hatzinakos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniDecVAEs框架，通过模态条件时频潜子空间和多视图自监督分解损失，在多模态可穿戴计算中同时实现分类、解耦表征学习、融合与生成建模，在最高30个模态的HAR任务中超越Transformer和VAE基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法作为全栈可穿戴处理器运作，即不能同时满足任务分类性能、解耦可解释表征学习、融合及高度异构多模态时间序列的生成建模需求，存在明显的技术空白。

Method: OmniDecVAEs扩展了DecVAEs框架，通过学习模态条件的时频潜子空间，利用多视图自监督分解损失和共享非对称自编码器（AE）架构，以统一且可扩展的方式从任意数量的模态中高效学习多用途表征。

Result: 在多达30个模态的omni-modal HAR任务中：活动识别准确率提升1.01%，身份识别准确率提升6.75%；重建误差（MAE）改善76.84%，真实与合成数据分布相似度（MMD）改善13.85%；模型仅4.1M参数，具备实时推理延迟。

Conclusion: OmniDecVAEs作为一种轻量级模型，凭借增强的表征能力、模态不变的空间复杂度和实时延迟，适合智能边缘可穿戴设备和临床医疗应用，以单一模型统一处理需求和能力。

Abstract: Learning disentangled representations is a key requirement for developing versatile, general-purpose, and sustainable models in multi-modal wearable computing. However, existing approaches do not operate as full-stack wearable processors, i.e., they do not simultaneously address task-specific classification performance, disentangled and interpretable representation learning, fusion, and generative modeling of highly heterogeneous multi-modal time series. To address this gap, we introduce Omni-modal Variational Decomposition Autoencoders (OmniDecVAEs), a framework that efficiently learns multi-purpose representations in a unified and scalable manner from arbitrarily many modalities. OmniDecVAEs extend DecVAEs by learning modality-conditioned time-frequency latent subspaces through a multi-view self-supervised decomposition loss and a shared asymmetric autoencoder (AE) architecture. Results on a challenging omni-modal human activity recognition (HAR) setting with up to thirty modalities, demonstrate the ability of OmniDecVAEs to learn full-stack wearable representations. When compared to transformer-based and VAE-based methods, OmniDecVAEs full-stack disentangled representation properties lead to accuracy improvements of 1.01% and 6.75% in activity and identity recognition, respectively. Furthermore, OmniDecVAEs synthesize realistic omni-modal time-frequency data that manifest with enhanced reconstructions (mean absolute error improves by 76.84%) and distributional similarity between real and synthetic data (maximum mean discrepancy improves by 13.85%). Our results highlight OmniDecVAEs potential as a lightweight model suitable for intelligent edge wearables and clinical healthcare, unifying processing requirements and abilities in a single model, through its enhanced representational capacity, modality-invariant spatial complexity (4.1M parameters), and real-time latency.

</details>


### [114] [Risk-Aware Decision Policies for Agents Under Noisy Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06420)
*David Szczecina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于人工生命的捕食者-猎物模型来研究噪声感知下的觅食决策，比较了多种考虑噪声预测的策略，发现不确定感知策略显著优于盲目信任感知标签的方法，并观察到行为从探索性向保守性的质变转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统的感知本质上带有噪声，生物体需要在不确定性下做出决策，而错误分类可能代价高昂甚至致命。现有的强化学习和人工生命研究较少系统地探讨感知噪声对觅食行为的影响，以及不确定性感知策略的优势。

Method: 构建了一个人工生命的捕食者-猎物觅食模型，在对称和非对称感知噪声条件下进行受控实验，对比多种策略：包括盲目信任感知标签的策略，以及显式利用噪声预测不确定性（如概率置信度）的策略。通过模拟分析不同噪声水平下代理的存活率、致命错误率和行为模式。

Result: 实验表明：随着噪声增加，盲目信任感知标签的策略导致灾难性失败；不确定性感知策略显著提高存活率并减少致命错误；观察到行为上的定性相变，代理从低噪声下的探索性策略转变为高噪声下的保守性策略。

Conclusion: 该模型将风险敏感觅食、生态信息利用和人工生命联系起来，表明显式信息收集可以在感知不可靠时提升鲁棒性，强调了不确定性感知决策的重要性，为噪声标签下的鲁棒学习提供了可解释的人工生命类比。

Abstract: Perception in biological systems is inherently noisy, requiring organisms to make decisions under uncertainty where misclassification can be costly or fatal. We present an Artificial Life predator-prey model of foraging under noisy perception, and compare agent performance when using various policies that take into account their noisy predictions. Through controlled experiments under both symmetric and asymmetric perceptual noise, we show that blindly trusting perceptual labels leads to catastrophic failure as noise increases, while uncertainty-aware strategies significantly improve survival and reduce fatal errors. We further observe qualitative regime shifts in behaviour, with agents transitioning from exploratory to conservative strategies as uncertainty increases. Our model links risk-sensitive foraging, ecological information use, and Artificial Life by showing that explicit information gathering can improve robustness when perception is unreliable. These results highlight the importance of uncertainty-aware decision-making and provide an interpretable artificial life analogue to robust learning with noisy labels.

</details>


### [115] [Sharding Prevents LLM Oversight Failures and Adversarial Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06422)
*Victor Akinwande,J. Zico Kolter,Aran Nayebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM作为裁判（judge）在批量评估多个要求时出现的"过载"问题：单次调用返回的判定数量越多，其判定与专家的一致性越低；即使该调用获得与一组独立调用相同的计算预算。作者提出"分片"（sharding）干预：将要求划分为小组、每组单独调用并聚合结果，可在保持模型、证据、总预算和单决策预算不变的情况下提升与专家的一致性。分片后的较弱裁判可胜过更强的整体裁判，并在对抗场景下表现出鲁棒性；但对逐条说服型攻击需叠加辩论式对立（debate-style opposition）才能抵御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM裁判在单次调用中需要同时给出大量判定（verdicts），这可能导致某些判定缺乏充分的证据支撑。尽管增加计算量，并不必然带来更多要求的核查，甚至可能降低判定可靠性。作者希望探索一种能在不增加预算的前提下提升LLM裁判可靠性的机制。

Method: （1）在专家评分的研究复现、法律工作和临床试验评估等多领域进行实验，对比单次调用返回多个判定与分组调用的效果；（2）提出"分片"（sharding）干预：将需求划分成小组，每组单独调用LLM裁判并聚合判定，同时保持模型、证据、总预算和每决策预算不变；（3）测试对抗场景：best-of-N对手通过仅改变呈现方式（不改变底层工作）诱导过载裁判接受未满足的标准；（4）在逐条件攻击场景下，叠加辩论式对立（debate-style opposition）来抵御自适应再优化的攻击者。

Result: （1）随单次调用返回的判定数量增加，与专家的一致性下降，横跨多个专业领域成立；（2）分片在固定全部资源下显著提升与专家的一致性；（3）分片的较弱裁判可在同预算下胜过更强的整体裁判；（4）best-of-N对手可通过仅改变表述将过载裁判对未满足标准的误接受率提高数倍，而分片能消除这种对抗优势；（5）分片不能抵御逐条件说服型攻击，但叠加辩论式对立后可有效应对自适应再优化的攻击者。

Conclusion: 模型监督（model-based oversight）中的裁决"过载"是真实且可量化的失败模式。分片作为简洁、预算中性的干预措施，能显著提升LLM裁判的可靠性并赋予对抗鲁棒性；对于更精细的逐条攻击，需要更复杂的辩论式机制补充。这为LLM在审查、合规和法律等高可靠性场景中的部署提供了切实可行的改进路径。

Abstract: Giving an LLM judge more compute does not necessarily make it check more requirements. When one call must return many verdicts, some decisions become weakly grounded in the evidence, even when that call receives the same token or tool budget as a panel of separate calls. Across expert-graded research replications, legal work, and clinical-trial assessments, agreement with experts falls as the number of verdicts per call grows. We identify sharding as the intervention that mitigates this failure in model-based oversight. Sharding partitions the requirements into smaller groups, assigns each group to a separate call, and aggregates the verdicts. Against a single call with the panel's full budget, sharding improves agreement while holding the model, evidence, total budget, and per-decision budget fixed. Overall, we find that a sharded weaker judge can outperform a more capable holistic judge and match that judge even when the latter receives the panel's full budget. Additionally, we find that sharding exhibits robustness against adversaries. A best-of-N adversary can hold the underlying work fixed, vary only its presentation, and increase an overloaded judge's acceptance of genuinely unmet criteria severalfold. Wherever sharding reduces baseline error, it removes this adversarial advantage, keeping over-acceptance low even as the adversary's search widens. Sharding does not address attacks that persuade the judge separately on each criterion rather than exploiting overload. In that setting, we find that debate-style opposition on top of sharding withstands such adaptive re-optimization.

</details>


### [116] [Adversarial Causal Intervention Falsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06427)
*Mojtaba Eslami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了对抗性因果干预伪造（ACIF）框架，将因果生成模型与对抗性实验者之间的序贯博弈形式化，用于通过干预-后验分布的验证来区分观测拟合、干预等价性和结构因果模型的点识别三个概念，并给出了理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型可以精确重现观测分布，但其内部编码的因果结构可能是错误的。现有的生成式对抗网络（GAN）判别器仅充当真实与合成样本的分类器，无法区分观测分布等价但因果结构不同的模型。作者希望通过引入干预性对抗实验者来检验生成模型对干预后分布的复现能力，从而揭示并认证模型背后的因果结构。

Method: 提出ACIF框架：结构因果生成器提出观测和干预分布，对抗性实验者选择能最大程度伪造生成器的干预。形式上，判别器被索引为干预条件下检验生成器是否复现对应干预后分布。论文形式化了oracle版本和可实现版本的游戏，并证明：对抗目标可精确简化为最坏干预下的积分概率度量（IPM）；在可分离干预族下可实现点到干预等价性的识别；混合策略纳什均衡的存在性；有限样本一致收敛与基于间隔的模型选择保证；在平衡分离条件下分歧驱动的序贯设计具有对数消除保证。此外给出了完整的线性高斯示例。

Result: 主要理论结果包括：(i) 对抗目标精确简化为最坏干预IPM；(ii) 干预等价性下的识别，分离干预族下点识别；(iii) 混合策略均衡的存在性；(iv) 有限样本均匀收敛与间隔模型选择保证；(v) 平衡分离条件下的对数消除保证。线性高斯示例表明两个观测不可区分的因果方向可通过单个精心选择的干预被分离。

Conclusion: 该框架厘清了对抗性因果判别器能够和不能够认证的内容，区分了观测拟合、干预等价性和结构因果模型点识别三个常被混淆的概念，为因果生成建模、主动因果发现和实验设计之间提供了原则性桥梁。

Abstract: Generative models can reproduce an observational distribution while encoding an incorrect causal structure. We study a sequential game in which a structural causal generator proposes observational and interventional distributions, while an adversarial experimentalist selects interventions intended to maximally falsify the generator. The discriminator is therefore not merely a real-versus-synthetic classifier: it is indexed by an intervention and tests whether the generator reproduces the corresponding post-intervention law. We introduce Adversarial Causal Intervention Falsification (ACIF), formulate oracle and implementable versions of the game, and distinguish three objects that are often conflated: observational fit, interventional equivalence over an admissible query class, and point identification of a structural causal model. For finite model and intervention classes, we prove: (i) an exact reduction of the adversarial objective to a worst-intervention integral probability metric; (ii) identification up to interventional equivalence, with point identification under a separating intervention family; (iii) existence of mixed-strategy equilibria; (iv) finite-sample uniform convergence and margin-based model-selection guarantees; and (v) a logarithmic elimination guarantee for a disagreement-driven sequential design under a balanced-separation condition. We also give a complete linear-Gaussian example in which two observationally indistinguishable causal directions are separated by a single well-chosen intervention. The framework clarifies what an adversarial causal discriminator can and cannot certify, and provides a principled bridge between causal generative modeling, active causal discovery, and experimental design.

</details>


### [117] [Fixed and Adaptive Topological DeepONets: Functional Measurements on Hausdorff Locally Convex Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06428)
*Khemraj Shukla,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Topological DeepONet框架基础上，将输入函数的点采样替换为Hausdorff局部凸空间连续对偶V'中的连续线性泛函，结合Lee-Shin系数空间两步法和自适应功能测量系统，构建了固定与自适应两种功能测量DeepONet。在多个算子学习任务（反导数算子、异质Darcy流、受控算子、Navier-Stokes涡量算子）上验证了方法的有效性，实现了分辨率无关的误差和解离散化可移植的连续对偶坐标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DeepONet通过固定离散化上的点值编码输入函数，受限于离散化和网格分辨率。Ismailov的Topological DeepONet框架提供更一般化的函数编码思路，但尚未构建实用的测量系统与学习算法。本文旨在将输入编码从点采样推广到更广泛的连续线性泛函，支持非范数化的局部凸输入空间，并发展自适应的功能测量以提高效率与精度。

Method: (1) 将Topological DeepONet中的点采样替换为Hausdorff局部凸空间(V,{p_α})连续对偶V'中的连续线性泛函作为功能测量；(2) 提出固定测量系统与自适应测量系统两种方案；(3) 结合Lee-Shin系数空间Two-Step训练过程，并引入仅训练阶段使用的解码器与正则化来稳定自适应坐标；(4) 推导离散误差分解（测量误差、输出基误差、神经网络逼近误差）及Barron-rate精细化分析。

Result: 在异质Darcy流动问题中，功能模型在未见网格上保持近乎分辨率无关的5.5-5.6%误差；受控算子问题中自适应测量将平均误差降至1.2%以下；固定时间Navier-Stokes问题中自适应Topological DeepONet达到1.685%±0.017%的平均相对L2误差（128个功能坐标），优于可比尺寸的FNO（0.832%±0.172%）除外，但后者需完整64×64输入场、2倍训练时间及10.7倍峰值GPU内存。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了紧凑、可解释、可移植于离散化的连续对偶V'中的坐标表示，尤其适用于非范数化的输入空间。自适应功能测量在多个问题中优于固定测量，且功能型坐标以更低计算成本在精度上与常规神经算子模型具有竞争力。

Abstract: Deep Operator Networks (DeepONets; arXiv:1910.03193) typically encode an input function through point values on a fixed discretization. Building on the Topological DeepONet framework of Ismailov (arXiv:2603.11972), we replace point samples by continuous linear functionals drawn from the continuous dual of a Hausdorff locally convex space $({V},\{p_α\}_{α\in A})$, whose topology is generated by a point-separating family of seminorms rather than a single norm, and develop fixed and adaptive functional measurement systems. Measurements are combined with the coefficient-space Two-Step procedure of Lee and Shin (arXiv:2309.01020), while a training-only decoder and regularization stabilize the adaptive coordinates. We derive a discrete error decomposition separating measurement, output-basis, and neural-approximation errors, together with a Barron-rate refinement. The framework is evaluated on the antiderivative operator, a non-normable locally convex input space, heterogeneous Darcy flow, a controlled operator, and fixed-time and time-evolving Navier-Stokes vorticity operators. In the heterogeneous Darcy problem, the functional models retain nearly resolution-independent errors of 5.5-5.6% on unseen grids, while in the controlled problem adaptive measurements reduce the mean error below 1.2%. For the fixed-time Navier-Stokes problem, the Adaptive Topological DeepONet is the most accurate DeepONet-based model, attaining a mean relative $L^2$ error of 1.685% +/- 0.017% using 128 functional coordinates. A comparably sized Fourier neural operator (FNO; arXiv:2010.08895) achieves the lower error 0.832% +/- 0.172%, but requires the full 64x64 input field, twice the training time, and 10.7x greater peak GPU memory. The formulation provides compact, interpretable, and discretization-portable coordinates in the continuous dual $V'$, including for non-normable input spaces.

</details>


### [118] [MiGHT-EHR: A Multi-task Graph Transformer for Heterogeneous Temporal Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06430)
*Anirudh Rayas,Yuan Wang,Pavan Turaga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MiGHT-EHR，一种用于电子健康记录（EHR）的多任务图Transformer框架，通过异构图表征同时建模临床实体异质性、纵向患者轨迹和跨任务统计依赖，在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR数据复杂多样，包含异构临床实体及其交互、纵向患者轨迹和跨任务共享的统计依赖，而现有EHR学习方法仅捕获了这些属性中的一部分，缺乏统一的表示学习框架来同时建模所有属性。

Method: MiGHT-EHR从EHR构建异构图，节点代表临床实体（患者、就诊、诊断、处方、程序），通过归一化逐点互信息（NPMI）连接统计关联的实体；采用多任务图Transformer在统一表示学习中联合建模异构实体、时序轨迹和任务间共享依赖。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV数据集上，MiGHT-EHR在药物推荐、住院时长预测、死亡率和再入院预测四个任务的平均表现上优于最先进方法，尤其在死亡率和再入院预测上提升显著。事后分析表明患者邻域按临床结局组织、可恢复线性方向上的关键医学概念、任务概率校准良好。

Conclusion: MiGHT-EHR的表示学习能够支持多样化的临床预测任务，同时保留临床上可解释的结构，为EHR统一表示学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Learning from Electronic Health Records (EHRs) has gained significant attention due to its potential to improve clinical prediction. However, effective learning remains challenging because EHRs encode heterogeneous, temporally ordered clinical interactions. In particular, EHRs contain: (i) heterogeneous clinical entities, including patients, visits, diagnoses, prescriptions, and procedures, together with their heterogeneous interactions, (ii) longitudinal patient trajectories across hospital visits and (iii) shared statistical dependencies across related clinical prediction tasks. Existing EHR learning methods capture only a subset of these properties. To bridge this gap, we propose Multi-task Graph transformer for Heterogeneous Temporal EHRs (MiGHT-EHR), which jointly models all three within a unified representation learning method. MiGHT-EHR constructs a heterogeneous graph from EHRs in which nodes represent clinical entities and edges connect statistically associated entities identified via normalized point-wise mutual information. Across MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV datasets, MiGHT-EHR outperforms state-of-the-art methods on average across four tasks: drug recommendation, prediction of length-of-stay, mortality, and readmission, with particularly strong improvements in mortality and readmission prediction. Furthermore, a post-hoc analysis of the learned representations reveals that patient neighborhoods are organized by clinical outcomes, salient medical concepts are recoverable as linear directions in the representation space, and task probabilities are well calibrated. Collectively, these findings demonstrate that MiGHT-EHR representations support diverse prediction tasks while preserving clinically interpretable structure.

</details>


### [119] [SNI-GNN: SmartNIC-Assisted Full-Graph GNN Training with In-Network Embedding Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06441)
*Guofan Yu,Sitian Chen,Zhenheng Tang,Xiaowen Chu,Amelie Chi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SNI-GNN是一个基于SmartNIC辅助的全图GNN训练系统，通过在网络内（in-network）预测远端嵌入来减少通信开销，同时保持模型精度。系统在NVIDIA BlueField-3上实现，与现有全图训练系统集成后，通信量减少21-45%，端到端加速1.3-3.6倍，精度损失≤0.01，可扩展到16个GPU、千万级边的图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全图GNN训练虽然精度高，但在多服务器集群上由于节点间频繁且不规则的嵌入交换导致扩展性差。现有的分区和压缩技术无法充分解决通信瓶颈，需要新的方法在不牺牲精度的前提下减少通信开销。

Method: SNI-GNN采用三重机制：1) 在SmartNIC上部署轻量级线性趋势预测器，基于缓存的历史嵌入精炼远端嵌入预测；2) 基于重要性（importance-based）的边界节点采样策略；3) 异步DPU-GPU数据流水线配合中间结果复用。同时提供了误差与收敛界理论分析，证明预测器偏差在二阶有界动态下可控，且在非精确梯度下保持标准非凸收敛。

Result: 在NVIDIA BlueField-3上实现，与SOTA全图系统集成后：通信量减少21-45%，相对BNS-GCN实现1.3-3.6倍端到端加速，相对基线SANCUS实现最高1.29倍加速，精度损失不超过0.01，可扩展到16个GPU、千万级边的图。

Conclusion: SmartNIC上的网络内预测是全图GNN训练中分区与压缩技术的实用补充方案，能够在大规模场景下有效实现通信高效的训练。

Abstract: Full-graph GNN training delivers high accuracy but scales poorly on multi-server clusters due to heavy, irregular inter-node embedding exchanges. We present SNI-GNN, a SmartNIC-assisted full-graph training system that reduces communication while preserving accuracy by predicting remote embeddings in-network. SNI-GNN deploys a lightweight linear-trend predictor on SmartNICs to refine cached historical embeddings, coupled with an importance-based boundary-node sampling policy and an asynchronous DPU--GPU data pipeline with intermediate-result reuse. We provide error and convergence bounds showing that predictor bias remains controlled under bounded second-order dynamics and yields standard non-convex convergence with inexact gradients. Implemented on NVIDIA BlueField-3, SNI-GNN integrates with state-of-the-art full-graph systems, cuts communication by 21--45\%, achieves 1.3--3.6$\times$ end-to-end speedups over BNS-GCN and up to 1.29$\times$ over baseline SANCUS, with accuracy loss $\leq 0.01$, and scales efficiently to 16 GPUs on graphs with up to tens of millions of edges. These results indicate SmartNIC-based in-network prediction is a practical complement to partitioning and compression techniques for communication-efficient full-graph GNN training at scale.

</details>


### [120] [ED-CSP: Crystal Structure Prediction from Electron Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06448)
*Germain Poloudenny,Yaël Frégier,Arnaud Demortière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ED-CSP，一个从稀疏、未索引的电子衍射（ED）观测中预测周期性3D晶体结构的机器学习框架，结合关系集编码器、置换不变多视图聚合和周期流生成器，联合预测晶格参数和分数原子坐标。在2,075个CHILI-100K材料上MR@5达57.49%，优于PXRDGen（52.92%）；扩展训练数据后MR@5提升至66.27%，并在新鲜组成上达到53.52%，验证了真实生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏、未索引的电子衍射观测中恢复周期性3D晶体结构是一个具有挑战性的生成式反问题。现有基于ED的学习方法主要预测晶体学标签、从索引反射重构结构或从有限结构库中检索候选，缺乏直接从原始ED观测进行生成式结构预测的能力。

Method: ED-CSP框架包含三个核心组件：1）关系集编码器（relational set encoder）用于编码ED斑点集；2）置换不变多视图聚合（permutation-invariant multi-view aggregation）整合多个探测器平面的信息；3）周期流生成器（periodic flow generator）联合预测晶格参数和分数原子坐标。训练数据ED-CS包含485万模拟多视图ED晶体结构，跨七个材料库去重并过滤CHILI-100K重叠。

Result: 在2,075个held-out CHILI-100K材料上，仅用CHILI训练的ED-CSP达到57.49% MR@5，超过PXRDGen（52.92%）。用百万结构预训练初始化后MR@5提升至66.27%。在1,024个训练检索库之外的新组成上仍达53.52% MR@5。用同组成非同构结构的衍射替换目标ED观测后，MR@5下降22.09个百分点，证明预测依赖输入衍射图案而非仅组成。

Conclusion: ED-CSP和ED-CS建立了从稀疏ED观测进行生成式晶体结构预测的基准，展示了超越精确公式检索的真实生成能力，为未来迁移到实验数据奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recovering a periodic 3D crystal structure from sparse, unindexed electron diffraction (ED) observations is a challenging generative inverse problem. Existing ED-based learning methods mainly predict crystallographic labels, reconstruct structures from indexed reflections, or retrieve candidates from finite structure libraries. Here, we introduce ED-CSP, a machine learning framework that predicts crystal structures from chemical composition, atom count, and multiple detector-plane ED spot sets. ED-CSP combines a relational set encoder, permutation-invariant multi-view aggregation, and a periodic flow generator to jointly predict lattice parameters and fractional atomic coordinates.
  To train the model, we construct ED-CS, a dataset of 4.85 million simulated multi-view ED crystal structures, deduplicated across seven materials repositories and filtered to exclude CHILI-100K overlaps. On 2,075 held-out CHILI-100K materials, ED-CSP trained only on CHILI achieves a structural match rate of 57.49% MR@5, outperforming PXRDGen (52.92%), a state-of-the-art crystal structure prediction model conditioned on powder X-ray diffraction. Scaling training data further improves performance: initializing from a one-million-structure precursor raises MR@5 to 66.27%. On 1,024 compositions absent from the training retrieval library, the model still achieves 53.52% MR@5, demonstrating true generative capability beyond exact-formula retrieval. Replacing target ED observations with diffraction from non-isomorphic structures of identical composition decreases MR@5 by 22.09 percentage points, confirming that predictions depend on the input diffraction patterns rather than composition alone. ED-CSP and ED-CS establish a benchmark for generative crystal structure prediction from sparse ED observations and provide a foundation for future transfer to experimental data.

</details>


### [121] [Beyond Attention: Signed Integrated Gradients Attribution in a BiomeGPT-Style Microbiome Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06486)
*Oren Nelson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对特征令牌化Transformer（如BiomeGPT）的可解释性问题，提出使用Integrated Gradients（积分梯度）替代传统的[CLS]注意力权重进行归因，并提出源相关基线T'=S+A₀来分离物种身份与丰度变量的贡献，实现病原/保护信号的极性区分及二阶Hessian社区交互分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征令牌化Transformer（如BiomeGPT）中，[CLS]注意力权重作为下游分类解释工具存在两个关键局限：(1) 注意力权重非负，无法区分疾病支持证据与健康支持证据；(2) 注意力权重作用于令牌融合之后，掩盖了输入源S（物种身份）和A（丰度）各自对输出的独立影响。因此需要一种有符号的、融合感知的归因方法。

Method: 采用Integrated Gradients（积分梯度），一种有符号且融合感知的归因方法。提出源相关基线T'=S+A₀，其中S为固定物种身份、A₀为基准丰度，在保留物种作为固定生物学坐标的同时隔离丰度变异的影响。将该方法应用于疾病与健康决策边界，获得能显式分离病原与保护微生物信号的极性。进一步推荐二阶Integrated Hessians来揭示微生物群落成员间的相互作用规则。

Result: 该梯度方法揭示了物种-丰度方向性关系及敏感性诊断，这些是无符号[CLS]注意力权重完全无法捕捉的信息。二阶Hessian能够暴露群落中一个成员的扰动如何改变模型对另一成员的敏感性，以及哪些物种在高丰度下引导模糊案例偏向疾病或健康。

Conclusion: 该方法为BiomeGPT的因果归因提供了有原则的解释框架，可推广到其他平滑可微的特征令牌化Transformer，并已开源代码。

Abstract: In a feature-tokenized transformer (arXiv:2106.11959) such as BiomeGPT (doi:10.64898/2026.01.05.697599), each input token is built by fusing a fixed identity with a sample-specific measurement: a fixed species and a variable abundance, T = S + A. To interpret downstream classification in such models, prior work inspects the attention weights of the special [CLS] token (arXiv:2106.11959, arXiv:1810.04805, BiomeGPT) to rank sample tokens by importance. These weights have two critical limitations: they are nonnegative, so they cannot separate disease-supporting from health-supporting evidence (arXiv:2201.12114), and they act after token fusion, obscuring how the input sources S and A each affect the output.
  To address this we use Integrated Gradients (arXiv:1703.01365), a signed, fusion-aware attribution method, and propose a source-derived baseline T' = S + A_0 for feature-tokenized models such as BiomeGPT, which preserves species identity as a fixed biological coordinate while isolating the effect of abundance variation. Applied to a disease-versus-health decision margin, it yields polarity that explicitly separates pathogenic from protective microbial signals. We show that this gradient-based approach uncovers species-abundance directional relationships and sensitivity diagnostics entirely obscured by unsigned [CLS] attention weights. We further recommend second-order Integrated Hessians (arXiv:2002.04138) to expose microbiome community interaction rules: how a perturbation in one member alters the model's sensitivity to another, and which other species drive ambiguous cases toward disease or health at a given abundance level. This provides a principled approach to explainability in BiomeGPT that generalizes to other smooth and differentiable feature-tokenized transformers. Code is available at https://github.com/nohren/token-source-attribution

</details>


### [122] [Toward Reliable Context Compression for Long-Horizon Agents: An Empirical Study of Execution Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06503)
*Guanghui Min,Liang Wu,Mayank Darbari,Chen Chen,Liangjie Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文初步研究了循环上下文压缩对长时程智能体行为的影响，发现压缩会削弱近期交互的影响力，导致更多阻塞动作、重复探索和运行间不稳定。为此提出TRACE框架，通过成对闭环延续进行边界局部评估，利用摘要偏好优化自然语言压缩提示，在AppWorld上超越现有压缩基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环上下文压缩虽能控制长时程智能体的上下文增长，但其行为影响尚未被充分理解。作者观察到压缩会削弱近期交互的影响，导致阻塞动作增加、重复探索增多以及跨运行不稳定，亟需一种可靠的压缩评估与优化方法。

Method: 提出TRACE框架：通过从同一环境状态进行配对的闭环延续来评估单个压缩事件（边界局部评估），并利用摘要偏好优化自然语言压缩提示，同时保持所有模型参数冻结。

Result: 在AppWorld基准上的初步结果显示，TRACE在任务性能、多运行可靠性和上下文-执行效率方面均优于现有压缩基线。

Conclusion: 这些发现为边界局部评估作为可靠智能体上下文压缩的有效方向提供了早期证据，暗示该方向值得进一步探索。

Abstract: Recurrent context compression controls context growth in long-horizon agents, but its behavioral effects remain poorly understood. In this preliminary empirical study, we show that compression can weaken the influence of recent interactions, increasing blocked actions, repeated exploration, and instability across runs. Motivated by these observations, we introduce TRACE, a verifier-guided framework that evaluates individual compaction events through paired closed-loop continuations from the same environment state and uses summary preferences to optimize a natural-language compression prompt while keeping all models frozen. Initial results on AppWorld show improvements over existing compression baselines in task performance, multi-run reliability, and context--execution efficiency. These findings provide early evidence for boundary-local evaluation as a promising direction for reliable agent context compression.

</details>


### [123] [Unmasking Removal-Budget Confounding: A Matched Operating-Point Evaluation Framework for Adaptive Data Cleaning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06511)
*Wei-Hsiang Chen,Pin-Hsuan Yu,Chen-Hsuan Fang,Jung-Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种操作点感知（operating-point-aware）的评估框架，用于解决自适应数据清洗方法中存在的"移除预算混杂"（removal-budget confounding）评估偏差，并通过多线索自适应清洗器的重新设计实验证明，多数表面性能提升在匹配操作点后消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应数据清洗方法用数据驱动的分区替代人工过滤阈值，但改变分区粒度或分组数量会隐式移动决策边界，改变移除样本总数，导致评估时出现移除预算混杂偏差——指标看似提升实际只是移除了更少的样本，而非真正具备更优的污染判别能力。现有评估方式缺乏对这种偏差的控制。

Method: 提出操作点感知评估框架，使用匹配预算（matched-budget）和匹配召回率（matched-recall）对照，并结合阈值无关指标（AUROC、AUPRC）。在此基础上，设计了一个多线索自适应清洗器，包含加权学习难度线索、辅助欧氏距离线索以及增加的分区粒度，用于隔离"干净但难"样本，并使用错误分解（false-positive decomposition）分析不同类型样本在不同污染率下的贡献。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-100上，朴素评估（在各配置自身操作点下评估）显示重新设计有显著提升，但匹配操作点后大部分提升消失或缩小。真正优势仅保留在特定低流行率场景和严重污染下的高召回区域。错误分解显示，"干净但难"样本在低污染率下主导错误计数，在中度污染下变得阈值依赖，在严重污染下贡献可忽略。

Conclusion: 自适应清洗方法必须在匹配的操作点下进行基准测试，才能确保性能提升反映真实的污染判别能力，而非移除预算差异带来的假象。

Abstract: Adaptive data-cleaning methods replace manual filtering thresholds with data-driven partitions. However, changing the partition granularity, the number of groups used to segment samples by estimated corruption risk, can implicitly shift the decision boundary and alter the overall number of removed samples. This creates a bias known as removal-budget confounding, where apparent gains in metrics like precision or false-positive rate reflect a smaller removal budget rather than superior corruption discrimination. To address this evaluation bias, we introduce an operating-point-aware evaluation framework that evaluates methods using matched-budget and matched-recall controls alongside threshold-independent metrics (AUROC and AUPRC). We test this framework on a multi-cue adaptive cleaner redesign featuring a reweighted learning-difficulty cue, an auxiliary Euclidean-distance cue, and increased partition granularity intended to isolate clean-but-difficult samples. While naive evaluations (assessing configurations at their own induced operating points) suggest substantial performance improvements for the redesign, these gains disappear once operating points are equalized. False-positive decomposition reveals that clean-but-difficult samples primarily drive error counts at low corruption rates, become threshold-dependent at moderate corruption, and contribute negligibly under severe corruption. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-100 demonstrate that most performance differences observed in naive evaluation shrink or vanish at low-to-moderate corruption when operating points are matched. True ranking advantages only remain in specific low-prevalence settings and in high-recall regions under severe corruption. These findings highlight that adaptive cleaning methods must be benchmarked at matched operating points to ensure performance gains reflect genuine corruption discrimination.

</details>


### [124] [Target-Weighted Neyman Allocation: Experimental Design for Heterogeneous Treatment Effects under Population Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06512)
*Hoang Dang,Luan Pham,Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TWNA（Target-Weighted Neyman Allocation），一种两阶段分层实验设计，利用试点估计的组-臂结果方差来分配最终阶段样本量和处理概率，以优化目标加权组平均处理效应（GATE）的估计精度，并在部署组成不确定时保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机实验通常在某一群体中进行，但结果却用于指导另一群体的决策。按实验比例分配会浪费预算在部署中很少出现的群体上，而按部署比例分配则对难以精确测量的群体样本不足。需要在部署重要性与统计难度之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出两阶段分层设计TWNA：第一阶段使用试点估计各组-臂结果方差，据此分配第二阶段样本量和处理概率。Oracle规则具有闭式解，在部署权重与统计难度之间取得平衡；plug-in规则在试点方差估计稳定后恢复Oracle规则。同时扩展TWNA处理部署组成的不确定性，并区分权重鲁棒性与面向偏态、稀有事件或污染结果的试点鲁棒变体。

Result: 通过模拟和真实协变量基准测试表明，当群体同时具有部署重要性和测量难度时，TWNA带来的增益最大。

Conclusion: TWNA在部署目标加权GATE精度方面显著优于固定比例分配方案，并能在部署组成未知或近似已知的情况下保持鲁棒性，为跨群体决策的实验设计提供了理论完备且实用的方法。

Abstract: Randomized experiments are often run in one population to guide decisions in another. Allocating by experimental proportions wastes budget on groups that rarely appear in deployment, whereas allocating by deployment proportions under-samples groups that are hard to measure precisely. We propose \textbf{TWNA} (Target-Weighted Neyman Allocation), a two-stage stratified design that uses pilot estimates of group--arm outcome variances to allocate final-stage sample sizes and treatment probabilities for target-weighted group average treatment effect (GATE) precision. The oracle rule has a closed form and balances deployment importance with statistical difficulty; the plug-in rule recovers it as pilot variance estimates stabilize. We also extend TWNA to handle uncertainty about deployment composition, remaining robust whether the target mix is roughly known or entirely unknown. Finally, we distinguish this weight robustness from a pilot-robust variant for skewed, rare-event, or contaminated outcomes. Simulations and real-covariate benchmarks show the largest gains when groups are both deployment-important and difficult to measure.

</details>


### [125] [CertBind from Multimodal Connectivity to Certifiable Retrieval Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06516)
*Shuheng Cao,Zhenhao Zhang,Ruiqi Chen,Renjie Cao,Weijia Zhang,Siyu Zhang,Jiaxin Liu,Xiangyu Zeng,Haotian Geng,Fan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CertBind 提出了一个多尺度理论框架，用于对冻结多模态编码器的连接图进行可认证组合。它通过节点、边、路径和查询四个尺度的分析，为连接的表示和任务决策提供可证明的保障，最终支持 Direct、Recovery、Certified 和 Abstain 四种路由决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级连接器使冻结的多模态编码器在表示层面可以实现组合，但部署时面临任务决策层面的新问题：连接路由可能扩展跨模态能力，同时改变原有检索性能。现有方法缺乏对组合后任务决策可证明性的理论保障。

Method: CertBind 采用四个尺度的理论框架：（1）节点尺度：原生锚点建立精确的任务识别边界；（2）边尺度：契约感知的共形秩提供图级族系误差控制；（3）路径尺度：重叠感知预算和干净校准提供有限样本恢复半径；（4）查询尺度：恢复半径产生覆盖 top-k 候选集，当集合大小等于 k 时成为点证书。系统据此将受支持路由标记为 Direct，仅将标记路由送入恢复，恢复决定性时返回 Certified，未解决查询返回 Abstain。

Result: 在 C-MCR 共享路由上，CertBind 将原生 CLIP 的 R@1 从 0.524 降至 0.290（连接引入的偏差）。生产环境回退恢复到干净检索的 0.963 ± 0.002，无损害分支的无损害值为 1.000。

Conclusion: CertBind 将多模态可组合性从连接的表示层面扩展到了可认证的任务决策层面，为连接模型在任务层面的安全部署提供了理论保证和实用机制。

Abstract: Lightweight connectors make frozen multimodal encoders composable at the representation level. Deployment exposes a second problem at the level of task decisions. A connected route can expand cross-modal reach while changing an established native retrieval capability. We introduce CertBind, a multiscale theory of certifiable composition for frozen multimodal connector graphs. At the node scale, native anchors establish the exact task identification boundary under the stated chart model. At the edge scale, contract-aware conformal ranks provide graph-wide family-wise error control. At the path scale, an overlap-aware budget and clean calibration yield a finite-sample recovery radius under declared conditions. At the query scale, this radius yields a covered top-k candidate set that becomes a point certificate when its size equals k. CertBind therefore retains supported routes as Direct, sends only flagged routes to recovery, returns Certified for decisive recovery, and returns Abstain for unresolved queries. The evaluated C-MCR shared route reduced native CLIP R@1 from 0.524 to 0.290. The production fallback recovered 0.963 +- 0.002 of clean retrieval, while the passing branch recorded a no-harm value of 1.000. CertBind extends multimodal composability from connected representations to certifiable task decisions.

</details>


### [126] [Online Security Learning in Cooperative Multi-Agent Systems under Hidden Byzantine Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06520)
*Ximing Sun,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体系统在拜占庭攻击下的在线协同控制问题，刻画了攻击者信息对鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）几何结构的影响，证明了安全遗憾的信息论下界，并提出一种阶段绑定的鲁棒估计到决策学习算法，实现O~(H²S√AK)+E[D_K]的安全遗憾上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体协同控制系统在现实部署中面临恶意节点的安全威胁，其中拜占庭攻击者可以观察并覆写团队计划动作。现有工作未系统研究攻击者信息（能否观察计划动作）对问题几何结构的影响，也未给出安全学习的信息论极限，缺乏在安全约束下进行在线学习与决策的理论与算法基础。

Method: 作者首先从问题建模入手：观察到攻击者能否看见计划动作决定了鲁棒MDP的表达形式——有观察攻击者诱导精确的(s,a)-矩形鲁棒MDP（行是覆写诱导的公开结果分布的凸包），而盲目攻击者诱导s-矩形模型。接着通过构造两个在单时段不可区分的实例来证明安全遗憾的Ω(K)下界（此时回报遗憾为零），说明对累计响应间隙D_K的依赖不可消除。最后提出阶段绑定的鲁棒估计到决策（regret estimation-to-decisions）学习器，将估计与决策在每个阶段绑定以应对攻击者的对抗性覆写。

Result: 该论文获得了三方面结果：(1) 刻画了攻击者信息对鲁棒MDP几何结构的影响，即有观察攻击者对应(s,a)-矩形凸包行模型，盲目攻击者对应s-矩形模型；(2) 建立了安全学习的信息论极限，证明两个不可区分的单时段时间隔实例迫使Ω(K)的期望安全遗憾，同时回报遗憾为零，表明对D_K的依赖不可避免；(3) 提出的阶段绑定鲁棒估计到决策算法实现了Õ(H²S√AK)+E[D_K]的安全遗憾界。

Conclusion: 该工作为拜占庭攻击下可靠多智能体系统提供了全面的理论和算法基础：攻击者信息决定几何结构，安全遗憾的信息论下界明确了学习困难的本质来源，所提出的算法实现了与依赖D_K的最优下界相匹配的性能，为实际安全多智能体协同控制提供了设计依据。

Abstract: We study online cooperative control of a multi-agent system under Byzantine attacks. Namely, an unknown, fixed subset of agents are Byzantine comprised and can stealthily overwrite its own coordinates of the team's planned joint action after observing that plan. The learner observes planned actions, public rewards, and public states, but neither the overwrite nor the executed joint action. Our objective is security: to optimize the team performance against the worst overwrites and achieve the optimal security value. We first show that the attacker's information determines the geometry. An attacker that observes the planned action induces an exact $(s,a)$-rectangular robust Markov decision process (MDP) whose rows are convex hulls of overwrite-induced public-outcome laws, whereas a blind attacker induces an $s$-rectangular model. We then identify the information-theoretic limit of security learning, showing that the security regret decomposes exactly into return regret against the response generating the data and a cumulative response gap $D_K$. Two indistinguishable horizon-one instances force $Ω(K)$ expected security regret while return regret is zero, showing that dependence on $D_K$ is unavoidable. Finally, we develop a stage-tied robust estimation-to-decisions learner and prove a regret bound of $\widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left(H^2S\sqrt{AK}\right)+\mathbb E[D_K]$. Our studies thus provide comprehensive theoretical and algorithmic foundations of reliable multi-agent systems under Byzantine attacks.

</details>


### [127] [Newton-Schulz Retraction-Based Inference Enables Hidden Quantum Markov Models to Outperform Classical HMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06554)
*Ning Ning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NS-RIS算法，一种基于牛顿-舒尔茨正交化的Stiefel流形推理方法，用于高效学习保迹隐量子马尔可夫模型（HQMM），首次证明HQMM能在非量子生成的数据上显著超越EM训练的HMM，并在多种基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）在复杂隐动态场景下能力受限；隐量子马尔可夫模型（HQMM）作为其量子推广，理论上具更丰富的隐表示能力，但现有HQMM学习方法在非量子过程生成的数据上始终未能一致优于EM训练的HMM，严重限制了其实际应用价值。

Method: NS-RIS采用牛顿-舒尔茨正交化来计算极因子搜索方向，同时保持Stiefel流形可行性，避免了昂贵的矩阵分解运算。算法在标准假设（光滑性、随机梯度、有限牛顿-舒尔茨精度）下建立了有限时间平稳性保证。

Result: 在合成HMM基准上，NS-RIS平均提升评估指标38.5%，最高提升50.6%，优于EM和最先进的HQMM方法COSM；在合成HQMM基准上，较COSM测试指标提升18.9%且运行时间降低12.0%；在真实Splice分类基准上，隐维度6和8时分别将平均分类误差较COSM降低17.9%和14.9%。

Conclusion: NS-RIS提供了首个HQMM能在非量子生成数据上显著优于EM训练的HMM的基准证据，推动HQMM从HMM的理论推广转变为实际可用的、具有表现力的科学序列数据建模工具。

Abstract: Hidden Markov models (HMMs) are widely used probabilistic models for discrete sequential data but can be limited when hidden dynamics are complex. Hidden quantum Markov models (HQMMs) generalize HMMs by replacing probability vectors with density matrices and stochastic transitions with quantum operations, enabling richer latent representations. However, existing HQMM learning methods have not consistently outperformed Expectation--Maximization (EM)-trained HMMs on data not generated by quantum processes, limiting their practical applicability. We introduce NS-RIS, Newton--Schulz Retraction-based Inference on the Stiefel manifold, a scalable algorithm for learning trace-preserving HQMMs. NS-RIS uses Newton--Schulz orthogonalization to compute a polar-factor search direction while preserving Stiefel-manifold feasibility, avoiding costly matrix decompositions. We further establish a finite-time stationarity guarantee under standard assumptions on smoothness, stochastic gradients, and finite Newton--Schulz accuracy. Empirically, NS-RIS provides the first benchmark evidence that an HQMM can significantly outperform an EM-trained HMM on data not generated by a quantum model. On synthetic HMM-generated benchmarks, NS-RIS outperforms both EM and the state-of-the-art HQMM method COSM, improving the evaluation metric by an average of 38.5% and by up to 50.6%. On a synthetic HQMM benchmark, it improves the test metric over COSM by 18.9% while reducing runtime by 12.0%. On the real-world Splice classification benchmark, NS-RIS also surpasses both EM and COSM in higher-dimensional latent regimes, reducing mean classification error by 17.9% for latent dimension 6 and 14.9% for latent dimension 8 relative to COSM. These results move HQMMs beyond a theoretical generalization of HMMs and establish them as practical and expressive models for scientific sequence data.

</details>


### [128] [Bootstrap-Conditioned Action Selection with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06559)
*Devansh Gupta,Shiv Tavker,Dmitry Efimov,Suchitra Sathyanarayana,Gitanjali Bhutani,Boris N. Oreshkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BC-ICL策略，利用预训练表格基础模型的上下文学习能力，通过自助重采样历史交互数据来生成随机化策略，用于在线上下文赌博机决策，在标准和在线协议下显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文赌博机虽然为样本高效个性化提供了自然框架，但在实际部署中面临稀疏且有偏的交互数据、不可靠的不确定性估计以及严重的冷启动问题。现有方法在这些条件下表现不佳，需要探索新途径来构建鲁棒的在线决策策略。

Method: 提出BC-ICL（Bootstrap-conditioned action selection using ICL）方法：每轮从交互历史中进行自助重采样，将该重采样作为冻结的预训练ICL模型的输入条件，对所有动作进行评分，并选择评分最高的动作。同时引入臂上下文（arm-context）条件架构，促进各动作间共享统计强度，避免孤立的臂赌博机的自助法失效模式。

Result: 该策略在标准上下文字典赌博机数据集上展现出强劲的早期回合后悔度和总体后悔度表现，在严格的在线协议下优于既定基线。

Conclusion: 预训练表格基础模型结合上下文学习的自助采样方法可以被有效转化为随机化在线决策策略，为实际部署中稀疏数据和冷启动问题提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Contextual bandits offer a natural framework for sample-efficient personalization, but practical deployment remains difficult under sparse, biased interaction data, unreliable uncertainty estimates, and severe cold starts. We study whether pre-trained tabular foundation models with in-context learning can be turned into randomized policies for online decision making. We propose BC-ICL (Bootstrap-conditioned action selection using ICL), which at each round draws a bootstrap resample of the interaction history, conditions a frozen pre-trained ICL model on that resample, scores all actions, and selects the action with the highest sampled score. We further introduce an arm-context conditioning architecture that promotes shared statistical strength across actions and helps avoid common bootstrap failure modes of isolated-arm bandits. Empirically, this policy delivers strong early-round regret and regret performance on standard contextual bandit suites, outperforming established baselines under a strict online protocol.

</details>


### [129] [Theoretical Foundations of Communication-Efficient, Robust, and Practical Distributed and Federated Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06563)
*Grigory Malinovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文围绕联邦学习与分布式优化中理论与实践的七大挑战展开，提出了ProxSkip、Variance Reduced ProxSkip、部分客户端参与下的局部步长加速、服务器端步长与无放回采样、压缩梯度差、梯度差裁剪的拜占庭鲁棒性以及基于随机非对称链的低秩适应等系列方法，建立了在现实假设下紧致的理论保证，并通过数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大规模训练依赖经典优化原理，但分布式系统的约束要求重新审视这些基础。论文旨在弥合机器学习优化理论与分布式实践之间的鸿沟，解决联邦学习与分布式优化中的关键瓶颈，特别是在通信效率、异质性、部分参与、拜占庭鲁棒性和大规模模型微调等方面。

Method: (1) 提出ProxSkip，证明局部梯度步数可以加速通信；(2) 开发Variance Reduced ProxSkip，消除随机局部更新的邻域误差并平衡通信与局部计算；(3) 证明局部步数在部分客户端参与下仍保持通信加速；(4) 证明服务器端步长与无放回采样在异质性环境下改善收敛；(5) 针对Random Reshuffling展示压缩梯度差优于压缩梯度；(6) 使用梯度差裁剪同时实现拜占庭鲁棒性与部分参与；(7) 基于随机非对称链建立低秩适应的首个理论框架。

Result: 提出了新颖的算法框架，在现实假设下建立了紧致（sharp）的理论保证，并通过数值实验验证了理论与实践的一致性，覆盖了通信加速、方差的处理、异质性收敛、压缩策略、鲁棒性与大规模模型微调等关键问题。

Conclusion: 论文系统性地解决了联邦学习与分布式优化中理论与实践交叉的七个核心挑战，为通信加速局部更新、方差缩减、部分参与、异质性处理、梯度差压缩、鲁棒性以及低秩适应等方向提供了严谨的理论基础和有效的算法设计。

Abstract: Machine learning and optimization have advanced together, with practical demands motivating new theory and theoretical breakthroughs enabling new applications. Modern large-scale training relies on classical optimization principles, but the constraints of distributed systems require these foundations to be reconsidered. This thesis addresses seven challenges at the intersection of theory and practice, focusing on key bottlenecks in federated learning and distributed optimization. First, we introduce ProxSkip and prove that local gradient steps can accelerate communication, providing a theoretical foundation for this widely used heuristic. Second, we develop Variance Reduced ProxSkip, which eliminates the neighborhood error of stochastic local updates while balancing communication and local computation. Third, we show that local steps retain their communication acceleration under partial client participation. Fourth, we prove that server-side stepsizes and sampling without replacement improve convergence in heterogeneous settings. Fifth, for Random Reshuffling, we demonstrate that compressing gradient differences rather than gradients yields better theoretical and practical performance. Sixth, we establish that Byzantine robustness and partial participation can be achieved simultaneously using gradient-difference clipping. Finally, we develop the first theoretical framework for low-rank adaptation based on randomized asymmetric chains, providing new insights into fine-tuning large models. Across these contributions, we introduce novel algorithmic frameworks, establish sharp guarantees under realistic assumptions, and support the theory with numerical experiments.

</details>


### [130] [Quantization Damage Is Multiplicative, Not Additive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06564)
*Zekun Wu,Swati Dhiman,Adriano Koshiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了量化对大型语言模型决策行为的破坏是"静默的"——基准分数几乎不变，但模型的工具调用和安全性拒绝行为会受损。作者提出"margin shrinkage"（边际收缩）现象：量化不是添加固定噪声，而是将决策边际乘以一个随比特数下降的因子（4比特中位数0.86，3比特0.33，2比特0.00），从而大幅降低模型对自身决策的置信保护。基于此规律可预测每个决策的翻转概率，且预测误差极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化是LLM实际部署的方式，低于4比特时会损害模型性能，但此前无法回答一个关键问题：在给定比特宽度下，模型的哪些决策会发生变化。这种损害是静默的——压缩后的智能体会停止调用工具、丢失一半的安全拒绝能力，但基准分数几乎不变。先前工作假设量化添加近似固定大小的噪声，这意味着高置信决策应当是安全的，但这一假设从未被真正检验过。

Method: 作者直接测量决策本身而非基准分数。对于二选一决策，定义边际（margin）为模型所选选项的分数减去最佳备选选项的分数，在量化前后追踪该边际。实验覆盖8个模型家族的16个模型、三种量化方法和2到8比特的位宽。通过测量发现边际的乘法收缩规律，并拟合统计关系来预测决策翻转概率。同时进行了配对边际集的成本匹配实验，比较不同修复方案的成本效益。

Result: 量化不添加固定大小噪声，而是将边际乘以一个随比特宽度塌缩的因子（4比特中位数0.86，3比特0.33，2比特0.00），这种现象称为边际收缩。该收缩降低了较大边际提供的保护，模型自身的小偏差决定失败方向：3比特时工具调用决策崩塌为不作为，但工具选择不受影响。拟合关系在held-out决策上预测翻转率的误差中位数为1.8个百分点；逐决策的翻转概率是校准的不确定性估计（131,758个预测上ECE为0.004）。该规律在所有被测模型中成立，但各模型的常数不可迁移。成本匹配测试表明，没有比增加一个比特更便宜的修复手段。

Conclusion: 量化对LLM决策的损害遵循"边际收缩"乘法规律而非传统假设的加法噪声模型，这使得高置信决策也不安全。这一规律可用于逐决策预测量化导致的翻转行为，提供校准的不确定性估计，无需完整生成式评估即可评估哪些决策会失效。由于各模型常数不迁移，需逐模型逐位宽测量配对边际集。最终结论是：在成本约束下，最优策略是增加一个比特来保障模型行为。

Abstract: Quantization is how large language models are actually deployed, and below four bits it is known to hurt. What nobody can say is which of the model's decisions will change at a given bit-width. The damage is silent: a compressed agent stops calling its tools, then loses half its safety refusals, yet benchmark scores barely move. Prior work assumes quantization adds noise of a roughly fixed size, which would make confident decisions safe. We measure the decision itself instead. The margin of a two-way decision is the model's score for the option it picks minus the score of its best alternative; we track it before and after quantization across 16 models from 8 model families, three quantization methods, and bit-widths from 8 down to 2. Quantization does not add fixed-size noise to the margin. It multiplies the margin by a factor that collapses with bit-width (median 0.86 at 4 bits, 0.33 at 3, 0.00 at 2); we call this margin shrinkage. This contraction reduces the protection a large margin affords; the model's own small biases pick the direction of failure: at 3 bits the decision to call a tool collapses toward inaction while the choice of which tool is untouched. In fitted statistical comparison, additive-noise accounts never win on the damaged tool and safety decisions. The fitted relation predicts flip rates within a median of 1.8 percentage points on held-out decisions, though no flip was used in the fit; per decision, the predicted flip probabilities are calibrated uncertainty estimates (expected calibration error 0.004 over 131,758 predictions). The same form holds in every model we measure, but the constants are each model's own and do not transfer. A small paired margin set, measured per model and bit-width, estimates which decisions break without full generative evaluation; under our cost-matched tests, nothing repairs damage more cheaply than one more bit.

</details>


### [131] [CrystalGRPO: Target-Aligned and Coverage-Preserving Reinforcement Learning for Flow-Based Crystal Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06582)
*Kaixiang Su,Hongfei Xue,Qiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CrystalGRPO，一种面向晶体结构预测的对齐后训练框架，将ODE到SDE的强化学习策略扩展到联合坐标-晶格状态，结合MACE能量预测与StructureMatcher恢复得分，提供CrystalGRPO-Q（单次采样恢复）和CrystalGRPO-C（有限预算覆盖恢复）两种模式，在MP-20和MPTS-52数据集上显著提升Top-N恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的生成模型虽能高效产生晶体结构预测的候选结构，但其预训练目标并未直接优化下游的目标恢复。现有的强化学习后训练方法主要依赖能量奖励和仅坐标的随机策略，存在两个关键问题：预测能量无法识别参考多晶型，且奖励驱动的集中化会降低Top-N恢复所需的候选覆盖度。

Method: CrystalGRPO将现有ODE到SDE策略构建扩展到联合坐标-晶格状态空间，结合MACE预测能量与基于StructureMatcher的恢复得分作为奖励信号，并提出两种操作模式：Q模式优先单次采样恢复，C模式结合全轨迹参考正则化与覆盖感知的组优势（group advantage）以保持有限预算下的目标恢复能力。

Result: 在MP-20和MPTS-52数据集上，使用PXRDGen和OMatG两个骨干网络，两种变体在所有四种骨干-数据集设置下均相比仅坐标强化学习降低了单样本和二十样本的RMSE。CrystalGRPO-Q一致地提升Top-1性能，而CrystalGRPO-C在所有设置中实现更高的Top-20。

Conclusion: CrystalGRPO通过将强化学习后训练对齐到CSP的实际目标（目标恢复），并引入覆盖感知的奖励设计，有效解决了现有方法在单次采样与有限预算恢复之间的权衡问题，为基于流的晶体结构生成模型提供了一种更优的对齐训练范式。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models can efficiently produce candidate structures for crystal structure prediction (CSP), but their pretrained objectives do not directly optimize downstream target recovery. Reinforcement-learning post-training offers a flexible solution, yet existing approaches rely primarily on energy rewards and coordinate-only stochastic policies. Predicted energy does not identify the reference polymorph, while reward-driven concentration can reduce the candidate coverage required for Top-N recovery. We introduce CrystalGRPO, a CSP-aligned post-training framework that extends existing ODE-to-SDE policy constructions to the joint coordinate--lattice state. CrystalGRPO combines MACE-predicted energy with a StructureMatcher-based recovery score and provides two operating modes: CrystalGRPO-Q, which prioritizes single-draw recovery, and CrystalGRPO-C, which combines full-trajectory reference regularization with a coverage-aware group advantage to preserve finite-budget target recovery. Across MP-20 and MPTS-52 with PXRDGen and OMatG backbones, both variants reduce one- and twenty-sample RMSE relative to coordinate-only reinforcement in all four backbone--dataset settings. CrystalGRPO-Q consistently improves Top-1, whereas CrystalGRPO-C achieves a higher Top-20 across all settings.

</details>


### [132] [Learning Suffers More Than the Policy Class Under Partial Observability: A Closed-Form Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07228)
*Idil Gözel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在部分可观测的线性二次控制问题中，即使最优策略可表示且价值函数表达力足够，标准的actor-critic强化学习仍然无法收敛到最优解，原因是critic在学习中产生了偏差而非actor的表达能力受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为部分可观测性下RL性能差是因为智能体无法表示足够的策略。作者质疑这一归因，试图证明即使在策略可表示、值函数足够表达的情况下，学习本身仍可能失败，并且找到根本原因。

Method: 研究一个部分可观测的线性二次（LQ）问题，在其中标准actor-critic学习器可以闭式求解。推导出学习后的策略、成本和消除问题所需的唯一设计选择（即前瞻步长）的闭式表达式，并用深度强化学习实验验证预测。

Result: 默认设定下最优可表示策略比全观测理想控制器只贵10.4%，但学习收敛到的策略比最佳可表示策略差35%，并可以精确指出偏差来源：critic将不可观测状态导致的未解释变异误读为价值估计中的尖锐曲率，actor跟随该误差远离最优。改变前瞻距离可以消除问题，而给智能体增加对过去观测的记忆并无帮助。

Conclusion: 部分可观测性下RL失败的核心不是策略表达能力或价值函数表达力，而是critic学习过程中的系统性偏差。问题的解决方案在于调整学习器的前瞻距离而非增加记忆信息。

Abstract: When a reinforcement learning agent cannot observe the full state, we usually blame its policies: it cannot see enough to represent a good one. We show that in a solvable case the bigger problem lies elsewhere. Even when a good policy is available and the agent's value function is expressive enough to describe it exactly, learning still ends up somewhere far worse.
  We study a partially observed linear-quadratic problem in which a standard actor-critic learner can be solved in closed form. At our default setting the best policy the agent can represent is already close to optimal, costing 10.4% more than the ideal controller that observes everything. Learning does not find it. The algorithm instead comes to rest at a policy that is 35% worse than the best one available to it, and we can say exactly where and why.
  The cause is a bias in what the critic learns rather than a limit on what the actor can express. Because the agent cannot attribute what it sees to the part of the state it cannot observe, the critic misreads that unexplained variation as sharp curvature in its own value estimates, and the actor follows that error away from the optimum. We derive closed-form expressions for the resulting policy, for its cost, and for the one design choice that removes the problem, which is how far the learner looks ahead before trusting its own value estimates. Deep reinforcement learning experiments follow these predictions closely. Notably, giving the agent memory of past observations does not help, while changing how far it looks ahead does.

</details>


### [133] [Flowing Through States: Neural ODE Regularization for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06595)
*Mohamed Ghanem,Bernd Finkbeiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经ODE的正则化方法，通过显式建模潜在表征的动态演化（类比MDP轨迹与ODE流），使表征学习与环境动态对齐，并将其集成到Actor-Critic算法中，在Atari基准和gridworld环境中取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络处理序列决策任务时依赖环境状态的潜在表征，但潜在状态的转移通常是隐式的，与真实环境动态之间存在不对齐（misalignment）问题，导致表征学习无法准确反映环境演变规律。

Method: 将MDP轨迹与ODE流进行类比（当前状态完全决定后继状态），提出基于神经ODE的正则化方法，强制潜在嵌入遵循一致的ODE流，从而将表征学习与环境动态对齐。该方法可广泛适用于深度学习智能体，并在强化学习中集成到Actor-Critic算法（如A2C和PPO）。

Result: 在多种标准Atari基准上验证了该方法对A2C算法的增益，在gridworld环境中验证了对PPO算法的增益，均取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模潜在动态并施加ODE流约束，可以有效提升表征与环境动态的一致性，从而改善强化学习智能体的性能；该方法具有广泛的适用性和显著的实证效果。

Abstract: Neural networks applied to sequential decision-making tasks typically rely on latent representations of environment states. While environment dynamics dictate how semantic states evolve, the corresponding latent transitions are usually left implicit, creating a potential misalignment between the two. We propose to model latent dynamics explicitly by drawing an analogy between Markov decision process (MDP) trajectories and ordinary differential equation (ODE) flows: in both cases, the current state fully determines its successors. Building on this view, we introduce a neural ODE-based regularization method that enforces latent embeddings to follow consistent ODE flows, thereby aligning representation learning with environment dynamics. Although broadly applicable to deep learning agents, we demonstrate its effectiveness in reinforcement learning by integrating it into Actor-Critic algorithms. Our approach yields major performance gains across various standard Atari benchmarks for A2C and gridworld environments for PPO.

</details>


### [134] [Retrofitting Linear Attention into Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06628)
*Jinha Kim,Younghun Roh,Jaeyeon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出块混合注意力（block-hybrid attention），将线性注意力应用于扩散语言模型（dLLM）中先前块的注意力计算，同时保留当前去噪块内的精确softmax注意力，以实现推理加速。通过将LLaDA 2.1 (16B)的6/20个注意力层替换为线性注意力层（仅需~60小时后训练），模型在HumanEval上达到72.0%（基线75.6%）、MBPP+上63.0%（基线57.7%）、CMATH上86.7%（基线88.3%），同时实现高达1.7倍解码吞吐量提升并支持更多并发请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散语言模型虽然通过块级半自回归解码和KV缓存加速推理，但每个去噪步骤仍然需要关注所有先前块，反复产生前缀注意力开销。作者希望进一步通过将先前块的注意力线性化来消除这一瓶颈，加速dLLM推理。

Method: 提出块混合注意力机制：在当前激活的去噪块内保留精确softmax注意力，对先前块采用线性注意力。该方法可被回溯性地应用于预训练dLLM，仅需极小的后训练成本。作者将LLaDA 2.1中的6个注意力层替换为线性层（遵循LoLCAT方法），转换耗时约60小时。

Result: LLaDA-Hybrid在基准测试中保持性能：HumanEval 72.0% vs 75.6%，MBPP+ 63.0% vs 57.7%，CMATH 86.7% vs 88.3%。基于Triton实现，解码吞吐量最高提升1.7倍，且在内存耗尽前可支持更多并发请求。

Conclusion: 研究表明预训练的扩散语言模型可以被高效地线性化以加速推理，块混合注意力是一种有效的后训练转换方案，在几乎不损失性能的情况下实现显著推理加速，为大规模dLLM的高效部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) offer a promising alternative to autoregressive models by accelerating inference through parallel decoding. Recent dLLMs commonly use blockwise semi-autoregressive decoding, generating blocks autoregressively while denoising tokens within each active block in parallel.
